1b1(phal chen): @##//shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo sangs rgyas phal po che zhes

1b4(phal chen): rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing ni ci tsam zhig du bsam gyis

2a1(phal chen): @#//rnam par dag pa ni ji tsam zhig du bsam gyis mi khyab/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi de bzhin 
2a2(phal chen): gshegs pa'i sku gdung ni ji tsam zhig du bsam gy-is mi khyab/sangs rgyas bco ma ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas skye ba ni ji 
2a3(phal chen): tsam zhig du bsam gyis mi kyab pa/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dos rnams kyi/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku ni ji tsam zhig du bsaM

2b1(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi chags pa mi mnga' ba'i rnam par spyod pa ni ji tsam zhig du bsam gyis mi khyab/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis sangs rgyas kyi rnam par 
2b2(phal chen): thar pa ni ji tsam zhig du bas ma gyis mi khyab snyam pa dang /de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' de dag gi sems mkhyen pas thugs su chud nas/byang chub sems 
2b3(phal chen): dpa' ud pa la'i snying po//sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi brlabs kyis byin du brlabs so//sangs rgyas kyi ye shes kyis byin dang ldan par mdzad do//de bzhin gshegs pa'i 'od kyis 
2b4(phal chen): snang ba mdzad do//sangs rgyas kyi gzi brjid kyis yongs su rgyas par mdzad do//sangs rgyas gyi mi 'jigs pa la rab tu bzhag go/sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi dbyings la 'jug pa 
2b5(phal chen): khong du chud par mdzad do//sangs rgya kyi bdag nyid che ba nyid dang /rdzu 'phrul gyi yul rab tu rtogs par mdzad do//chags pa med pa'i rgyud 'byam klas pa la rab tu bzhag go//
2b6(phal chen): sangs rgyas thams cad gcig nas gcig tu brgyud pa'i rigs rab tu rtogs par mdzad//sangs rgyas kyi tshul gyi gnas brjod du med pa la rab tu gzhag pa mdzad do//de nas byang 
2b7(phal chen): chub sems dpa' ud pa la'i snying pas chos kyi dbyings la 'jug ba rig cing khong du chud pas chags pa med pa'i dbyings su sgrub pa'i yid dang /chos thams cad la sgrib pa med par 
2b8(phal chen): gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa dang /kun tu bzang po'i smon lam dang ldan pa'i yang dag par 'jug pa dang /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rjes su song ba'i sangs rgyas blta ba dang 
3a1(phal chen): @#//snying pos lta ba'i sems can thams cad yangs su sbyong ba'i go ca chen po dang /byang chub sems dpa'i yi dam dang /spyod pa'i dkyil 'khor rgyun chad pa 
3a2(phal chen): med pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi ye shes mngon par bsgrub pa skad cig gi cha shas la rnam par spyod pa dang /thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi sgo mthong ba mi zad pa dang /so so yang dag par 
3a3(phal chen): rig pa'i rnam pa gzungs thams cad kyis snang bar byas pa dang ldan pas sangs rgyas kyi mthus byang chub sems dpa' pad mo'i snying po la 'di skad ces smras so//kye rgyal 
3a4(phal chen): ba'i sras/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni/tshad med pa la rnam par spyod pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras/sangs rgyas bcom/ldan 'das rnams ni rnam par 'phrul ba chen po la 
3a5(phal chen): rnam par spyod pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras/sangs rgas bcom ldan 'das rnams na sangs rgyas kyi mdzad pa thams cad la/sku'i mtshan thams cad rab tu brnyes pa'o//sangs rgyas 
3a6(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba ni ting nge 'dzin dang /dgongs pas sbyar ba'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi so so yang dag par rig pa bzhi ni 
3a7(phal chen): brjod du med pa yang brjod du shin tu bstan pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams ni sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad la bsam gyis mi khyab par spyod pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom
3a8(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi gsung dang dbyangs ni tshad med pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom dang ldan 'das rnams kyi chos kyi dbyings thugs su chud pa ni brjod du med pa'o//
3b1(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi khyu mchog gi mthu byung ba ni thams cad mngon par mkhyen pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyis chos kyi 
3b2(phal chen): dbyings thugs su chud cing /bstan pa ni sgrib pa med pa'i mthar thug pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras/bcu po 'di dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/rnams kyi chos kyi dbyings mtha' dang 
3b3(phal chen): dbus med pa'i sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams te/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasrnams kyi sku gzugs kyi rnam par dag pa ni rnyog pa med par rgyud thams cad du byung bas mtha' 
3b4(phal chen): dang dbus med pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
3b5(phal chen): 'das rnams kyi chags pa med pa'i snyan ni 'gro ba thams cad kyi sgra sgrib pa med par thugs su chud pas mtha' dang dbus med pa'o//sang rgyas bcom ldan 
3b6(phal chen): 'das dbus med pa'o//sangasargyas bcom ldan 'das rnam gyi ljags shin tu yangs pa ni chos kyi dbyings thams 
3b7(phal chen): cad du gsung gi dbyangs rjes su 'gro bas mtha' dang dbus med 
3b8(phal chen): pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom dang ldan 'das rnams kyi sku'i chos nyid ni sems can thams cad ma lus pa'i sems kyi rnam pa 
4a1(phal chen): @#//la de bzhin gshegs pa mthong ba rnam par dmigs pas mtha' dang dbus med pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sku dang tha mi dad cing dus gsum 
4a2(phal chen): chags pa med par thugs su chud cing rnam par shes pa dang rnam par dag pas mtha' dang dbus medapa'o//sangs rgyas bcom dang ldan 'das rnams kyi rnam par thar pa sgrib pa mi mnga' 
4a3(phal chen): ba la rnam par spyod pa ni sangs rgyas kyi go 'phang dang rnam par 'phrul ba dang /cho 'phrul mi zad pa yang dag par ston pas mtha' dang dbus medaba'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas 
4a4(phal chen): rnams kyi 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad rnam par dag pa ni brjod du med pa'i rgyan byin gyis brlabs pas/sangs rgyas kyi zhing nye bar nyon mongs pa med pa yang dag par bstan te/
4a5(phal chen): bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du rnam par dmigs par mdzad pas mtha' dang dbus med:pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom lngan 'das rnams kyi byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa'i khyad par dang /
4a6(phal chen): smon lam thams cad yongs su rdzogs pa ni rnam par rol pa'i ye shes kyi chos yongs su rdzogs te/chos thams cad rdzogs par byang chub par bya ba la mtha' dang dbus med pa ste/
4a7(phal chen): kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyi dbyings mtha' dang dbus medapa'i sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams 
4a8(phal chen): so||kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu bo 'di dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi skad cig la ye shes mngon par sgrub pa yang dag par bstanapa'o//bcu gang zhe na 
4b1(phal chen): skad cig gcig la 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du dga' ldan gyi gnas nas 'pho ba yongs su bstan pa dang /skad cig gcig la 'jig rted gyi khams thams cad du byang chub sems 
4b2(phal chen): dpa'i che yongs su bstan pa dang/skad cig gcig la 'ji rten gyi khams thams cad mngon par 'byung ba yongs su bstan pa dang/skad cig gcig la 'jig rtan gyi khams thams 
4b3(phal chen): cad du byang chub kyi shing drung du bzhugs shing mngon par 'tshang rgya ba yongs su bstan pa dag skad cig gcig la 'jig rton gyi khams thams cad du chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba yongs su 
4b4(phal chen): bstan pa dang /skad cigagacig la 'jig rten gyi kham sa thams cad du 'dul ba'i sems can yongs su smin pa'i tshul ji lta ba bzhin du /dus la bab pa shes pas sangs rgyas pa snyen bkur 
4b5(phal chen): ba yongs su bstan pa dang/skad cig gcig la 'jigrten gyi khams thams cad sems can ji ltar 'dul ba'i tshul bzhin du de bzhin gshegs pa brjod du med pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi 
4b6(phal chen): sku sna tshogs rnam par dmigs pa yongs su bstan pa dang skad cig gcig la 'jigarten gyi khams thams cad du/rgyan sna tshogs dang rgyan brjod du med pas/de bzhin gshegs pa'i khyu 
4b7(phal chen): ma cig gi mthu phyung ba dang /thams cad mkhyen pa'i mdzod yongs su bstan pa dang/skad cig gcig la 'jiganyen gyi khams thams cad durnam par dag pa gcig ma yin pa thams cad 
4b8(phal chen): yongs su bstan pa dang /skad cig gcig la 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad 'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas skye ba thams cad du dbang po dang /brtson 'grus 
5a1(phal chen): @#//rnam pa tha dad pa byung ba dang /mos pa dang /spyod pa rnam pa tha dad pa byung pa las dus gsum gyi sangs rgyas kyi rgyud mngon par 'tshang rgya ba yongs su bstan pa ste/kye 
5a2(phal chen): rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi skad cig la ye shes mngon par bsgrub pa yang dag par bstan pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 
5a3(phal chen): 'di dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi dus las mi 'da' ba ste/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'od rnams kyi mngon par 'tshang rgya ba ni/de'i dus 
5a4(phal chen): las mi 'da' bas dus la bab pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dis rnams kyi dge ba rnam par smin pa dang /rnam par smin pa mnyam pa nyid ni de'i dus las mi 'da' bas dus la 
5a5(phal chen): bab pa yin/sangs rgyas pa bcom ldan 'das rnams gyis sems can gyi bsam pa yongs su smin pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams lung bstan pa ni de'i dus la bab pa yin/sang rgyas 
5a6(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis sems can gyi bsam pa'i rgyud du/cho 'phrul yongs su bstan pa ni de'i dus las mi 'ad bas dus la bab pa yin no/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
5a7(phal chen): 'das rnams kyis sems can/mos pa ji lta bu dang ldan pa'i tshul bzhin du sku yongs su bstan pa de ni de'i dus las mi 'das pas dus la bab pa yin//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams btang 
5a8(phal chen): snyoms su bzhugs pa ni de'i dus las mi 'gal bas dus la bab pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
5b1(phal chen): mi 'da' bas dus la bab pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams dad pa ji lta bu dang ldan pa'i tshul bzhin du sems can yongs su 'dzin pa na/de'i dus las mi 'da' bas dus 
5b2(phal chen): la bab pa yin/sangs rgyasabacom ldan 'das rnams sems can kun nas nyon mongs pa dang ldan zhing gdul dka' ba la gzhan la ltos pa mi mnga' bar 'dul ba ni de'i dus las mi 
5b3(phal chen): 'da' bas dus la bab payin/sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi rnam par 'phrul ba bsam gyis mi khyab pas/rnam par 'phrul ba bstan pa ni de'i dus las mi 'dal 
5b4(phal chen): bas dus la bab pa yin/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dis rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyis dus las mi 'da' ba'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu bo 'di dag ni sangs

5b6(phal chen): rgyas par dgang ba dang /dus cig tu sangs rgyas thams cad yongs su bstan pa dang /'od gzer gcig gis 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad snang bar bya ba dang /sku gzugs 
5b7(phal chen): gcig gis lus dang /gzugs thams cad yongs su bsten pa dang /gnas gcig na bzhugs kyang 'jig rten gyi khams thamasacad ma lus par byon pa'i skur 'gyur ba dang /ye 
5b8(phal chen): shes gcig rtogs pas chos thams cad la sgrib pa med par 'gyur ba dang /thugs gcig bskyed pas chos kyi dbyings thams cad ma lus parargyas par 'gengs pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi 
6a1(phal chen): @#//che ba nyid tshad med pa dang ldan pa'i gshegs stabs gcig gis de kho na nyid yang dag par bstan pa dang /dus gsum thams cad sangs rgyas kyi go 'phang gis rgyas par 
6a2(phal chen): 'gengs pa dang /'gro ba thams cad dang ma 'dres par bzhugs shing 'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams dang /mi dmigs pa'i tshul kyis 
6a3(phal chen): rgyud kyi tshul gcig pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da' rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi yul dpe med cing bsam gyis mi khyab pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras 
6a4(phal chen): bcu po 'di dag ni de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis brlab mngon par sgrub pa'i ye shes te/bcu gang zhe na/'jug pa med pa'i chos thams cad la 'jug pas 
6a5(phal chen): smon lam mngon par bsgrub pa'i ye shes dang /lus med pa la lus mngon par bsgrub pa'i ye shis dang /chos thams cad mi gnyis pa la mngon par 'tshang rgya ba mngon par bsgrub 
6a6(phal chen): pa'i ye shes dang /sems can gyi mthar thug par bdag med pa la sems can 'dul bar mngon par bsgrub pa'i ye shas dang /chos thams cad mtshan ma sna tshogs mngon par bsgrub 
6a7(phal chen): pa'i yeshes dang /'jig rten gyi khams kyi yul ma skyes pa la 'jig rten gyi khams 'du ba dang /'jig pa mngon par bsgrub pa'i yeshes dang /yul thams cad mngon par 'du bya ba 
6a8(phal chen): med pa la/las kyi rnam par smin pa mngon par bsgrub pa'i yi shes dang /chos thams cad brjod du med pa la/chos kyi dbyings su gsung mngon par bsgrub pa'i ye shes dang /chos 
6b1(phal chen): thams cad kun nas nyon mongs pa dang /rnam par byang bas 'jug pa med pa la kun nas nyon mongs pa dag/rnam par byang ba mngon par bsgrub pa'i ye shes dang /chos thams cad ma skyes pa 
6b2(phal chen): la skye ba dang /'jig rten pa'i mtshan nyid rten cing 'brel bar 'byung ba mngon par bsgrub pa' ye shes te/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi mngon par bsgrub 
6b3(phal chen): pa'i ye shes so//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu bo 'di dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi tshad med pa'i khongs su chud pa dag ste/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams 
6b4(phal chen): kyi sku ni dus gsum thams cad kyi rjes su 'brang ba'i khongs su chud pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams sems can thams cad 'dul bar mdzad pa ni/cho 'phrul chen po gsum 
6b5(phal chen): gyi khongs su chud pa yin//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/rnams tshig rab tu dbye ba thams cad la mkhas pa ni sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi gzungs thams cad kyi khongs su chud pa yin/sadas rgyas 
6b6(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis chos kyi 'khor lo thams cad ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i so so yang dag par rig pa bzhi'i khongs su chud pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dis rnams sems can

7a1(phal chen): @#//ldan 'das kyi chags pa mi mnga' bar spyod pa ni chos kyi dbyings thams cad khongs du chud pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi skad cig gcig tu ni 'das

7a7(phal chen): che ba'i chos bzod par bka' ba'o//sems can 'dul ba'i dbang gis 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad tu gshegs pa ni sangs rgyas bcom ltan 'das rnams kyi shin turgya che ba'i

7b1(phal chen): khams thams cad ma lus par skus khyab cing sangs rgyas kyi sku rnam par phye ba sna tshogs thams cad du/mi gnyis pa ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi shin tu rgya chen po'i chos 
7b2(phal chen): bzod par dka' ba'o//gsung gi yan lag thams cad la sems can mos par bya ba dang /thos par bya ba dang /gdul bar bya ba don yod par yang dag par rig pa thogs pa med pas bstan

7b4(phal chen): spu'i bu ga gcig nas de bzhin gshegs pa 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed bsam gyis mi khyab cing /thogs pa med pa'i tshul du 'jig rten gyi khams thams
7b5(phal chen): cad kyi rdul shin tu phra pa snyed kyi thabs kyis 'byung zhing de bzhin du sangs rgyas kyi sku'i ba spu'i bu ga thams cad nas 'byung ba ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi shin tu rgya che ba'i
7b6(phal chen): chos bzod par dka' ba'o //de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rdul shin tu phra ba gcig la sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'jig rten gyi khams tham sa 
7b7(phal chen): cad kyi rdul shin tu phrab snyed kyi yongs su ston cing /sangs rgyas kyi rgyan dang rang bzhin gyi lhab lhub dang /chos kyi 'khor lo skor brgyun mi 'chad cing sems can rnam par 'dul ba thogs
7b8(phal chen): pa med pa 'jig rten kyi khams de dag tu gtogs pa rnams la'ang mi bde ba dang /gnod pa med kyang rdul shin tu phra ba de'ang yangs par ma byas/'jig rten gyi khams de dag kyang chung bar ma
8a1(phal chen): @#//byas par ji ltar gnas pa'i chos kyi dbyings kyang thugs su chud pa'i ye shes mnga' ba ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi shin tu rgya che ba'i chos bzod par 
8a2(phal chen): dka' ba'o//mun pa med pa'i khams de bzhin gshegs pas mngon par sangs rgyas te/sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad la mun pa med cing sgrib pa mi mnga' la sems can pha rol kyi 
8a3(phal chen): sgrib pa sna tshogs kyi gnas rnam par sel na/de la de bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu dang ldan pa ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams shin tu rgya che ba'i chos 
8a4(phal chen): bzod par bka' ba ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi shin tu rgya che pa'i chos bzod par dka' ba'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag bcu po 'di dag ni/
8a5(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/rnams kyi bdag nyid che ba rnam pa thams cad du kha na ma tho ba med cing shin tu dag pa ste/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dis rnams kyi yang dag par 
8a6(phal chen): sgrubs pa bdag nyid che ba ni sngon gyi mtha' nas rnam pa thams cad du khan ma tho ba med cing rnam par dag pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi 'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da 
8a7(phal chen): ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas kyi rgyud du rigs dang ldan par 'byung ba'i bdag nyid che ba ni/de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs rnam par dag pas/rnam pa thams cad du kha nam tho ba med cing 
8a8(phal chen): shin tu rnam par dag pa'o//sang rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam ni rnam par shes pa la mi gnas pa ste/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
8b1(phal chen): zur thug par gnas pas/rnam pa thams cad du kha na ma tho ba med cing /shin tu rnam par dag pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni dus gsum la mi gnas par rnam par 
8b2(phal chen): spyod cing chos thams cad la rten par yang dag par dgra bcom pas sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi bdag nyid che ba ni/rnam pa thams cad du kho na ma tho ba med cing shin tu rnam 
8b3(phal chen): par dag pa'o//sangs rgyasabacom ldan 'das rnams nidangos po rnam pa tha dad pa la rnam par gzhag pa gcig gis len pa med pas sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi bdag 
8b4(phal chen): nyid che ba ni rnam pa thams cad du khan ma tho ba med cing shinatu rnam par dag pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das
8b5(phal chen): mnyam pa'i bsod nams mi zad pa dang ldan pas mi zad pa'i bsod nams mnga' ba ste/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
8b6(phal chen): 'das rnams kyi bdag nyid che bani rnam pa thams cad du la na ma tho ba med cing shun tu rnam par dag pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom
8b7(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams ni mi 'jigs pa bzhi brnyes pas bag tsha ma mi mnga' bar bzhugs shing /khyu mchog dang seng ge'i nga ro 'byin te rgol ba'i nang na stag lta bu mthu dang ldan pa la chos thams
9a1(phal chen): @#//cad bstan pa la thugs sgron ma lta bu mda' bas/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi bdag nyid che ba ni rnam pa thams cad du kha na ma tho ba med cing shin tu rnam 
9a2(phal chen): par dag pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni yongs su mya ngan las 'das nas bskal pa brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa zhig lon yang mtshan gyi tshig thos ba tsam 
9a3(phal chen): gyis bzhugs pa dang 'dra bar sems can rnams kyi yon tan shin tu sbyong zhing mya ngan las 'das nas de srid kyi yun ring po 'das kyang sangs rgyas la bskyed pa'i yon tan zad par 
9a4(phal chen): mi 'gyur bas/sangs rgyas bcom lnga na 'das rnams kyi bdag nyer gyi khams brjod du med pa'i yang pa brjod du med pa dag na bzhugs shing 'jig rten gyi khams rgyang ring po na bzhugs kyang 'jig rten gyi khams 'di sems can rnams 
9a5(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi khams brjod du med ba'i yang brjod du med par ston pa mdzad pas/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams kyi bdag nyid che ba ni rnam pa thams cad du kha na ma tho ba med cing shin tu rnam par dag pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i 
9a6(phal chen): dag pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi bdag nyid ches ba ni rnam pa thams cad da kha na ma tho ba med cing/shin tu rnam par dag pa'o||kye rgyal ba'i 
9a7(phal chen): sras dag bcu po 'di dag ni sangs rgyas bcom lngan 'das rnams kyi shin tu rnam par dag ba ste/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
9b1(phal chen): rnam par dag pa la sngon gyi smon lam gyis shin tu rnam par dag pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni chags pa med pa'i tshul khrims kyis shin tu rnam par dag ba yin/
9b2(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni sems can thams cad kyi nyon mongs pa sel bas shin tu rnam par dag payin/sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams ni sangs rgyas kyi zhing gis 
9b3(phal chen): shin tu rnam par dag pa yino/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'angs rnams ni 'khor gyis shin tu rnam par dgab yin/sangs rgyas bcom 
9b4(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams ni rigs rnam par dag pas shin tu rnam 
9b5(phal chen): par dag pa yin/sangs rgya bcom ldan 'das rnams nigazugs kyi sku'i mtshan dang /dpe byad bzang po rnam par dag pas shin du rnam par dag pa yin//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams 
9b6(phal chen): ni chos kyi sku rnyog pa med pas shin tu rnam par dag yin//sangs rgyas bcom 
9b7(phal chen): ldan 'dis rnams ni sangs rgyas kyirnam par thar pa thams cad la mthu chen po brnyes shing /pha rol tu phyin pa dam pa brnyes pas shin tu rnam par dadag pa yin te/kye rgyal 
9b8(phal chen): ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi shin tu rnam par dag pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni sangs rgyas bcom 
9b9(phal chen): ldan 'anas rnams kyi mdzad pa 'jig 
9b10(phal chen): rten gyi khams thams cad du 'byung zhing dus thams cad du gnas pa ste/bcug bzhin/sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa thob pa'i sems can rnams la dus thams cad du sangs rgyas bstan pa dang /
10a1(phal chen): @#//sems can sems can shes dang ldan pa rnams la/dus thams cad du chos bstan pa dang /sems can sangs rgyas pa dad pa'i sems dang ldan pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba 
10a2(phal chen): dus thams cad du bskyed pa dang /chos kyang skyon med par 'das pa'i sems can rnams dus thams cad du ye shes khong du chud par bya ba dang /'gro ba thams cad du sangs rgyas kyi mdzad 
10a3(phal chen): pa dus thams cad du rgyun mi 'chad pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa rnams 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du dus thams cad du/chags pa mi mnga' bar gshegs pa dang /
10a4(phal chen): sems can thams cad la thugs rje chen pos dus thams cad du mi gtong ba dang /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi sprul pa dus thams cad du thogs pa med pa 
10a5(phal chen): dang|sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da' rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi rnam par 'phrul pa sna tshogs dus thams cad du thogs med pa dang /sangs rgyas bcom 
10a6(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams chos kyi dbyings gnas pa'i sangs rgyas dus thams cad 'byung ba ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi mdzad pa 'jig rten gyi khams thamasacad du gnas 
10a7(phal chen): pa'o||kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po'i 'di dag ni de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyi ye shes rgya mtsho mi zad pa'i tshul rnams te/bcu gang 
10a8(phal chen): zhe na|sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dis rnams ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku ye shes rgya mtsho mi zad ba'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/rnams ni sangs rgyas kyi phrin las tshad med pas 
10b1(phal chen): bar+ny+nyis pas ye shes rgya mtsho mi zad pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni sangs rgyas kyi spyan gyi yul mnga' bas ye shes rgya mtsho mi zad ba'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams ni 
10b2(phal chen): dge ba'i rtsa ba tshad med pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa mnga' bas ye shes rgya ma tsho mi zad pa'o//sangs rgya bcom ldan 'das rnams na chos kyi char mngon par 'bab pas ye shes rgya mtsho mi 
10b3(phal chen): ma'o||sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni sangs rgyas kyi yon tan legs pa mang po mnga' bas ye shes rgya mtsha mi zad pa'o//sangs rgyasabacom ldan 'das rnams nisngon gyi mtha'i 
10b4(phal chen): mur thams cad mkhyen pa'i smon lam dang khyad par mnga' bas ye shes rgya mcho mi zad ba'o//sangs rgya bcom ldana'adas rnams niphyi ma'i mtha'i mur 'gro ba thams cad sangs rgyas kyi 
10b5(phal chen): phrin las rgyun mi 'chad par sgrub pas ye shes rgya mtsho mizad pa'o//sangs rgyas bcam ldan 'das rnams ni sems can gyi rigs thams cad ma lus pa'i sems dang spyod pa la ye 
10b6(phal chen): shes rgya mtsho mi zad pa'o//sangs rgas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni thams cad mkhyen pa'i bsod nams zil gyis mi non pas ye shes rgya mtsho me zad pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag 
10b7(phal chen): ni de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyi ye shes rgya mtsho mi zad pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu bo 'di dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das 
10b8(phal chen): rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi tshul rnams te/bcu gang zhe na/'di lta ste pha rol tu phyin pa thams cad kyis mngon par 'byung ba 'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi tshul yin no//
11a1(phal chen): @#//'di lta ste chos thams cad la mi mkhyen pa gang yang mi mnga' ba 'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi tshul yin no//'di lta ste chos thams chad kyang thugs rje 
11a2(phal chen): chen po dang ldan pa 'di ni sangs rgyas bcom dan 'das rnams kyi tshul yin//'di lta ste thams cad kyang stobs bcu tshad med pa dang ldan pa 'di ni sangasargyas bcom ldan 'das 
11a3(phal chen): rnams kyi tshul yin no//'di lta ste thams cad kyang bla na med pa'i chos kyi 'khor lo rab tu skor ba 'di ni sangs rgyas bcam ldan 'das rnams kyi tshulayin no//'di lta ste thams cad
11a4(phal chen): kyang sems can 'dul ba la yang dag par zhugs pa 'di ni sangs rgyas bcam dan 'das rnams kyi tshu la yin no//'di lta ste thamasacad kyang 'gro ba thams cad kyi don du mngon par 
11a5(phal chen): sangs rgyas pa 'di ni sangs rgyas pa bcom ldan 'das rnams gyi tshul yin no//'di lta sto 'dul ba'i sems can rnams 'dul ba la thogs pa mi mnga' ba 'di ni sangs rgyas bcom
11a6(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams kyi tshul yin no/'di lta ste mi gnyis par yang dag par spyod pa'i mtshan nyid kyis chos la rab tu gzhol ba 'di ni sangs rgyas pa tsam ldan 'das rnams kyi tshu yin no/
11a7(phal chen): 'di lta ste ji ltar yongs su smin par 'gyur ba dang /dul bar 'gyur ba'i sems can rnams la sangs rgyas kyi yul mu med pas sangs rgyas kyi yongs su mya ngan las 'dar ba ston pa 'di
11a8(phal chen): ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das kyi tshul yin te/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po bdag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan dag rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi tshul lo//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu bo 'di dag
11b1(phal chen): ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi chos mtha' yas pa shin tu bstan pa'i sgo rnams te/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni sems can gyi khams shin tu 
11b2(phal chen): bstan pa'i sgor mtha' yas pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasar rnams ni sems can gyi spyod pa sna tshogs shin tu bstan pa'i sgor mtha' yasapa'o//sangs rgyas bcom 
11b3(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams ni sems can thams cad kyi las dang rnam par smin pa shin tu bstan pa'i sgor ma tha' yas pa'o//sangs rgya bcom ldan 'das rnams ni semasacan thams cad la gdams ngag 
11b4(phal chen): dang man ngag shin tu bstan pa'i sgor mtho' yas pa'o//sangs rgyasabacom ldan 'das rnams nisems can mtha' yas pa rnam par sbyong ba'i nan tan gyi sgor mtha' yas pa'o//
11b5(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams ni byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa thams cad la gdams ngagadang man ngag gis yongs su gzhol ba'i sgor mtha' yas pa'o/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
11b6(phal chen): 'das rnams ni byang chub sem dpa' khyad par gyi smon lam rnam pa tha dad pas 'byung ba shin tu bstan pa'i sgor 
11b7(phal chen): mtha' yas pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams na 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad ma lus pa'i 'du ba dang 'jig pa shin tu bstan pa'i skor mtha' yas pa'o//sangs 
11b8(phal chen): rgyas bcom ldan 'das kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing rnam par dag pa ni byang chub sems 
11b9(phal chen): dpa'i bsam pa dang mos pa ji lta ba bzhin du yongs su bstan pa'i sgor mtha' yas pa'o//sangasargyas bcom ldan 'das rnam pa zhi 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad du 'das 
12a1(phal chen): @#//pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas gcig nas gcig tu brgyud pa na bskal pa yangs pa de dang de dag tu bstan cing brjod pa dang /yongs su bshad pas/
12a2(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi ye shes thams cad khong du chub par bya ba'i sgor mtha' yas pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi chos mthA 
12a3(phal chen): yas pa shin tu bstan pa'i sgo'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po'i de dag ni sangs rgyas bco ldan 'das rnams kyi sangasargyas kyi phrin las sems can rnams la bsgrub pa ste/bcu gang 
12a4(phal chen): zhe na|sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi sku yongs su bstan pa ni sems can rnams la sangs rgyas kyi phrin las bsgrub po//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/rnams kyi gsung 
12a5(phal chen): yang sems can rnams la sangs/rgyas kyi phrin las bsgrub bo//sangs rgyas bcom ldan bdas rnams len pa mang ba yang sems can rnams la sad rgyas kyi phrin las bsgrub/
12a6(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasrnams len pa mi mdzad pa yang sems can rnams la /sangs rgyas kyi phrin las sgrub po//sa dang chu dang me dang /rlung gis kyang sangs rgyas bcom 
12a7(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams sems can rnams la sang rgyas kyi phrin las bsgrubs po//sangs rgyas bcom dan 'das rnams ni byin gyis brlabs kyi yul dang mi dmigs pa thams cad yul 
12a8(phal chen): du mdzad pas kyang sems can rnams la sangsa rgyas kyi phrin las bsgrung ngo//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi mtshan kyang sems can rnams las sangs rgyas kyi phrin las
12b1(phal chen): bsgrub po//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni zhing la dmigs par mdzad pas kyang sems can rnams la sangs rgyas kyi phrin las bsgrub po//sangs rgyas bco ldan 'das 
12b2(phal chen): rnams ni sangs rgyas kyi zhing rnam par dag pas kyang sems can rnamasal sangs rgyas kyi phrin la bsgrub po//sangs rgyas pa com ldan 'das rnams mi gsung bar bzhugs pa yang 
12b3(phal chen): sems can rnams la sangs sargyas kyi phrin las bsgrub pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasakyi sangs rgyas kyi phrin lasasems can rnams la bsgrub 
12b4(phal chen): pa'o////bam po gnyis pa/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcupo 'di dag ni sangs rgyas bcomaldan 'das rnams kyi sems can gyi gtso bo'i chos te/bcu gang zhe na/smon lam chen po la rab 
12b5(phal chen): tu brten zhing smon lam zhig pa mi mnga' bas ji skad du gsung ba de ltar mdzad cing gnyis su mi gsung ba 'di ni sems can gyi gtso bo'i tshos dang po'o//phyi ma'i mtha'i mur thug pa'i bskal 
12b6(phal chen): pa thams cad du byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa la yon tan thams cad yongs su rdzogs par mdzad pas rab tu brtson zhing sgyid lug pa mi mnga' ba 'di ni sems can gyi btso bo'i chos 
12b7(phal chen): gnyis pa'o//sems can 'dul bar 'gyur ba gcig gcig gzigs kyang 'jig rten gyi khams brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med par gshegs shing 'jig rten gyi khams thams 
12b8(phal chen): cad du gshegs pa la thag thogs med pa 'di ni sems can gyi gtsau bo'i chos gsum pa'o//zhe sdang ba'i sems can rnams la thugs rje chen pos snyoms pa dang /dad pa'i sems can 
13a1(phal chen): @#//rnams la thugs rje chen pos snyoms pa 'di ni sems can gyi gtso bo'i chos bzhi pa'o//gang gi tshe bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu yang dag par zhugs 
13a2(phal chen): nas/bla na med pa yang dag pa rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par sangs rgyas kyi bar du de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs ba'i sangs rgyas su mi 'gyur ro//
13a3(phal chen): snyam du byang chub kyi sems ma nyams pa 'di ni sems can kyi gtso bo'i chos lnga pa'o//dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad yang dag par bsgrub cing /thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du yongs su 
13a4(phal chen): bsngo bas 'jug gi/'jig rten pa'i rgyud la ma yin pa 'di ni sems can gyis gtso bo'i chos drug pa'o//sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rjes su sngon sangs rgyas kyis spyad pa'i sems su 
13a5(phal chen): lus kyi las dang /ngag gi las dang /yid kyi las kyis slob cing nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas kyi spyod pas thams cad mkhyen pa nyid duyongs su bsngo ba med pas/bla na med pa yang 
13a6(phal chen): dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mdon par sangs rgyas pa 'di ni sems can gyi gtso bo'i chos bdun pa'o//byang chub sems dpa'i sems rnam par dag cing 'od gsal ba dang /thams 
13a7(phal chen): cad du snang zhing kun tu 'od 'byung ba dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad la snang zhing thams cad du snyoms pas/thams cad mkhyen pa nying yongs su rdzogs par byed pa 'di ni sems 
13a8(phal chen): can gyi gtso bo'i chos brgyad pa'o//'jig rten pa'i bde ba thams cad yongs su spangs te/'jig rten pa'i dga' ba thams cad la ma chags pa dang /'jig rten thams cad kyis 
13b1(phal chen): gos pa med pa'i smon lam thams cad du mnyam pa nyid kyis/'jig rten thams cad bde ba thams cad dang ldan par byas te/sdug bsngal dang spros pa thams cad rab tu 'joms pa 'di ni 
13b2(phal chen): sems can gyi gtso bo'i chos dgu pa'o//sdug bsngal gyi mi bde ba thams cad nyams su bab ste/'gro ba thams cad kyi don du dka' thub gyis shin tu gdungs kyang bla bar sems bskyed 
13b3(phal chen): pas/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rigs kyi nges su srung ba dang ldan pa dang /stobs bcu'i bar du sangs rgyas kyi yul la mos pas mthar thug pa 'di ni sems can gyi gtso bo'i cho 
13b4(phal chen): bcu pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sems can gyi gtso bo'i chos//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu bo 'di dag ni sangs rgyas bcom 
13b5(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi rnam par spyod pa chags pa med pa rnams te/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad du 
13b6(phal chen): gshegs pas chags pa mi mnga' bar rnam par spyod pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
13b7(phal chen): 'das rnams ni 'jig rten gyi kham thams cad na bzhugs pa dang bzhengs pa dang /bzhud pa chags pa mi mnga' bar rnal par spyod pa

14a1(phal chen): @#//'das rnams ni 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad kyi dga' ldan gyi gnas na bzhugs kyang chags pa mi mnga' bar rnam par spyod pa yin/sangs rgyas 
14a2(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams ni chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa thams cad ni sngon gyi mtha' dang /phyi ma'i mtha' dang /da ltar byung ba'i mtha' la chags pa mi mnga' bar rnal par spyod pa yin/
14a3(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni ches kyi dbyings ma lus pa thabs cad tsam gyi 'khor kyi dkyil 'khor na bzhugs kyang chags pa ma mnga' bar spyod pa yin/sangs rgyasabacom 
14a4(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams ni sems can ma lus pa thams cad kyi sems dang spyod pa la gzigs shing /thugs kyi skad gcig cig la chos 'phrul chen po gsum gyis sems can rnam par 
14a5(phal chen): 'dul yang chags pa mi mnga' bar ran par spyod pa yin/sangs rgyus bcom ldan 'das rnams su gcig gis kyang tshad med pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i sangs rgyas kyi rnam 
14a6(phal chen): par spyod pa chen por rnam par spyod kyang chags pa mi mnga' bar rnam par spyod pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni sems can gyi khams 'byam klas pa ma lus pa 
14a7(phal chen): thams cad du de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos ma lus pa thams cad kyis mthar thug pa bsam gyis mi khyab bar ston cing gsung yang chags pa mi mnga' par rnam par spyod pa yin te/
14a8(phal chen): kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnamasakyi sangs rgyas kyi rnam par spyod pa chags pa med pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po'adi dag ni sangs rgyas 
14b1(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi rgyan mchog dam pa bla na med pa rnams te/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis sku mtshan dang dpe byad bzang po sna tshogs kyis rnam 
14b2(phal chen): par brgyan pa ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dis rnams kyi sku'i rgyan mchog dam pa bla na mad pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi gsung yan lag gi mchog dam pa drug cu 
14b3(phal chen): dang ldan zhing yan lag lnga brgyas yongs su bzung ba dang /rgyan brgya stong du mas rab tu brgyan pa dang /dbyangs kyi yan lag mtha' yas mu med pas rnam par dag pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi 
14b4(phal chen): chos kyi don dang tshig dang yi ge brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pas rab tu tshim par mdzad pa dang /bag tsha ba mi mnga' bar seng ge'i nga ro chen po sgrogs pa dang /'jigs pa med pa chen 
14b5(phal chen): pos bsnyengs pa mi mnga' bar gyur pa dang /chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa thams cad sems can thams cad la grag cing stsal te/khong du chud par 'gyur ba dang /sems can bsam pa 
14b6(phal chen): tha dad pa dang /dbang po tha dad pa rnam pa sna tshogs 'dul bar nye bar gnas pa 'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi gsung gi rgyan mchog dam pa bla na med pa'o//sangs rgyas 
14b7(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis thugs kyi phrin las ni stobs bcus brgyan pa sangs rgyas kyi ting nge 'dzin chen po thams cad kyi me tog shin tu rgyas pa sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa 
14b8(phal chen): bco brgyad kyi rgyan gyis shin tu brgyan cing sgrubs pas chags pa med pa'i spyod yul dang /chos kyi dbyings kyi gzhi la rab tu rten pa/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi chos gting med pa rab tu 
15a1(phal chen): @#//brnyes pa/chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa thams cad kyi rgyan du gyur pa/dus gsum gyi chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa ma thams cad kyi sems can kun kyi sems dang spyod pa 
15a2(phal chen): thams cad skad cig gcig dang ldan pa'i shes rab kyis rab tu mkhyen pa ste/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi thugs kyi rgyan mchog dam pa bla na med pa'o//sangs rgyas 
15a3(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams ni 'od zer bye ba brjod du med pa rab tu 'gyed par mdzad de/'od zer de dag re re la yang 'od zer gyi 'khor gyi dra ba brjod du med pa dang ldan pa/sangs rgyas kyi 
15a4(phal chen): zhing 'byam klas pa thams cad ma lus par snang bar byed cing 'jig rten gyi khams kyi rgyud thams cad mun pa med par ston pa/sangs rgyas skye ba dang mi 'bral bas/sangs rgyas kyi 
15a5(phal chen): phrin las las phyir mi ldog cing don yod pa/sangs rgyas skye ba brjod du med pas gnas pa med pa rang bzhin rnam par dag cing kun tu snyoms par 'byung ba ste/'di ni sangs rgyas bcam 
15a6(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams kyi 'od zer gyi rgyan mchog dam pa bla na mad pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams bzhad mo 'dzum par ston na/zhal gyi sgo nas 'od zer kha dog sna tshogs kha 
15a7(phal chen): dog du ma dang ldan pa bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong brjod du med pa phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad snang bar byed pa sems can gyi khams tshad med grangs med 
15a8(phal chen): bsam gyi mi khyab pa rnam par ston pa/bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu rnam pa gzhan du mi 'gyur bar lung ston pa/'gro ba thams cad du shin tu gsal bar 'byung ste/
15b1(phal chen): 'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis zhal 'dzum pa bstan pa'i rgyan mchog dam pa bla na med pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi chos kyi sku ni tshad med pa ste/sgrib pa 
15b2(phal chen): med pas rnam par dag pa chos thams cad la chags pa med pa nyid kyis mthar thug pa mtha' dang dbus med pa'i chos kyi dbyings la gnas pa/'jig rten thams cad dang ma 'dres pa 'jig 
15b3(phal chen): rten thams cad dang 'dre ba med pa/'jig rten thams can kyi de kho na nyid la yang dag par zhugs pa/'jig rten thams cang las gyen du 'phags pa/'jig rten thams chad las 'das 
15b4(phal chen): pa'i chos kyis yang dag par spyod pa/tshig gi lam dang bral ba/brjod du med pa'i mthar phyin pa/phung po dang khams dang /skye mched bral ba ste/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom 
15b5(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams kyi chos kyi sku'i rgyan mchog dam pa bla ni med pa'o//sangs rgyas bcam ldan 'das rnams ni 'od tshad med pa mnga' ba ste/'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad du'ang ma 
15b6(phal chen): 'dres shing /rgyan du ma dang ldan pa kha dog sna tshogs brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa'i rgyan dang ldan pa'i 'od kyi snang ba kun tu thogs pa med cing /thams cad du chags pa med 
15b7(phal chen): pa 'od kyi dkyil 'khor sgrib pa med pa tshad med pa'i mdzod du gyur pa/'od thams cad kyi 'byung gnas su gyur pa ste/'di ni sangs rgyas bcam ldan 'dasarnams kyi rgyan mchog dam pa 
15b8(phal chen): bla na med pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni kha dog mtha' yas pa mnga' ba ste/kha dog yid du 'ongs mnga' ba'o//kha dog 'od gsal ba mnga' ba'o//kha dog tshad med pa mnga' ba'o//
16a1(phal chen): @#//sems can gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad kyi bsam pa dang /mos pa bzhin du rnam par dmigs pa'i kha dog mnga' ba'o//de ma yin pa'i kha dog gzhan 
16a2(phal chen): khams gsum du gtogs pa thams cad zil gyis gnon pa'i kha dog mnga' ba'o//kha dog thams cad kyi pha rol tu phyin pa brnyes pas sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni kha 
16a3(phal chen): dog bla na med pa mnga' ba ste/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da' rnams kyi kha dog gi rgyan mchog dam pa bla na med pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni dge bas shin tu 
16a4(phal chen): rnam par dag pa ste/'das pa dang ma byon pa dang da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rigs dge ba rin po che'i 'byung gnas su gyur pa la shin tu byung ba'o//chos nyid kyi 
16a5(phal chen): rnam pa thams cad du khyad par yod pa dang khyad par med pa'i spyod pas rnam par dag pa'i rgyan gyi ye shes skye ba thams cad kyi nang du gtogs pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs 
16a6(phal chen): sgrib pa med pa'i rgyud rnam par dag pas/'jig rten gyi mi snyan pa thams cad kyis mi snyan par brjod du med pa ste/'di ni sangs rgyas pa bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs 
16a7(phal chen): rgyas kyi rigs dang /rgyud rnam par dag pa'i rgyan mchog dam pa bla na mad pa'o//sangs rgyas bcomaldan 'das rnams ni byams pa chen po'i mthu dang rang bzhin gyi rgyan mnga' 
16a8(phal chen): ba ste|shin tu dang pas yongs su rtog pa'i rnyog pa mi mnga' ba sku'i phrin las yongs su bsal ba lta bas mi skyo ba/dgongs pa shin tu rnam par bsal ba thugs rje chen po 
16b1(phal chen): mnga' ba/'gro ba thams cad skyob pa yon tan gyi gnas kyi dam pa bla na med pa yongs su 'dzin pa'i nang na thugs brtse ba can phan par mdzad pa sems can thams cad kyi don tu ye 
16b2(phal chen): shes chen po byin gyis brlabs te/bsod nams chen pos yongs su 'phags pa 'gro ba thams cad kyi bsod nams kyi zhing du gyur pa sems can rnams kyi yon tan dang /ye 
16b3(phal chen): shes phung po thams cad rnam par 'phel bar mdzad pa ste/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi byams pa chen po dang /thugs rje chen po'i mthar thug pa'i bsod nams 
16b4(phal chen): kyi rgyan mchog dam pa bla na med pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
16b5(phal chen): 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi rgyan mchog dam pa bla ma med pa'o//kye rgyal ba'isras 
16b6(phal chen): bcu po 'di dag ni sangs rgyas bcom dam 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas mnga' chen pos mnga' bsgyur ba'i chos rnams te/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni 
16b7(phal chen): chos thams cad la mnga' ba dang /chos thams cad snang bar mdzad pa'i mnga' bsgyur bas/tshig dang yi ge mtha' yas pa la thogs pa mi mnga' ba'i spobs pa mnga' ba dang /chos 
16b8(phal chen): thams cad ma lus par yongs su brjod pas mnga' che ba ste/'di ni sangs rgyas pa bcom ldan 'das rnams gyi sangs rgyas kyi mnga' chen pos mnga' ba sgyur ba'i chos dang po'o//
16b9(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni sems can ji ltar yongs su smin pa dang /smon lam ji ltar btab pa'i tshul bzhin du/'dul ba la thogs pa mi mnga' bas mnga' che ba ste/'di 
17a1(phal chen): @#//sangs rgyas kyi mnga' chen pos mnga' bsgyur ba'i chos gnyis pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad sems 
17a2(phal chen): can gcig la yang gnod pa med par rnam pa drug du rab tu bskyod pa dang /'jig rten gyi khams rgyan rnam par bkod pa grangs med tshad med pa dang ldan pa nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mthas 
17a3(phal chen): gtugs pa thams cad gcig nas gcig tu 'phang pa dang /gcig nas gcig tu bzhug pa dang /gcig nas gci tu bsdu ba dang /gcig nas gcig du bsre bas 'jig rten gyi 
17a4(phal chen): khams gzhan dang gzhan dag gzhan gyi phyogs su rab tu bzhag kyang sems can gyi khams rnams 'khor zhing rmongs par mi 'gyur la/the tsom du mi 'gyur bar mdzad pas mnga' che 
17a5(phal chen): ba ste/'di ni sangs rgyas kyi mnga' chen pas mnga' bsgyur ba'i chos sum pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom dan 'das rnams ni 'jig rted kyi khams ma lus pa thams can byin gyis 
17a6(phal chen): brlob pa'i ye shes kyis 'jig rten gyi khams kyi rgyan phan tshun du byin gyis brlab pa dang /dgongs pa'i skad cig gis 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi rgyan yang dag par bstan 
17a7(phal chen): pa dang /bskal pa grangs med par brjod kyang yi ge shin tu bstan pa zad mi shes pa dang /'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad rgyan thams cad kyis rnam par brgyan 
17a8(phal chen): pa dang /mi mthun pa med cing nye bar nyon mongs pa med pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi zhing gcig tu yongs su 'du bar sangs rgyas kyi zhing rnam par dag pa thams cad byin gyis 
17b1(phal chen): brlabs pa la mnga' che ba ste/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi mnga' chen pos mnga' bsgyur ba'i chos bzhi pa'o////sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams 
17b2(phal chen): ni sangs rgyas kyis 'dul ba'i sems can gzig man sems can gcig gi don du bskal pa brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med par skyil mo krung gcig gis bzhugs na yang de 
17b3(phal chen): bzhin gshegs pa'i sku la mi bde ba mi mnga' zhing thugs la mi bde ba mi mnga' ste/gang zag de yang mi snyel bas 'dul ba'i dus las mi 'da' ba dang /gang zag gcig la ji lta 
17b4(phal chen): ba de bzhin du rim pa rim pas/'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad du phyi ma'i mtha' mur thug pa'i sku tshe la mnga' bar byin gyis brlab te bzhugs kyang /sangs rgyas     
17b5(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams la skyo ba mi mnga' ste/'di ltar sems can gyi khams thams cad mthar gyis rim pas sangs rgyas kyis 'dul pa'i gang zag gi don du yongs su bskyod  
17b6(phal chen): par mdzad pa la mnga' che ba ste/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi mnga' chen pos mnga' bsgyur ba'i chos lnga pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni 'jig 
17b7(phal chen): rten gyi khams ma lus pa thabs cad du yang dag par gzhol ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i bzhud pas 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du bzhud pa la thag thogs mi mnga' ba dang /
17b8(phal chen): phyogs bcu'i chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa thams cad kyi 'jig rten gyi khams kyi rgyud rgya mtsho 'byam klas pa dang /phyogs kyi dra ba'i rgyud thams cad du dgongs pa'i skad cig    
18a1(phal chen): @#//gcig dang ldan pa'i bzhud par byin gyis brlob cing /chos kyi dbyings shin tu bstan pa khong du chud par bya bas chos kyi 'khor lo thogs pa med 
18a2(phal chen): pa rab tu bskor ba la mda' che ba ste/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi mnga' chen pos mnga' bsgyur ba'i chos drug pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams 
18a3(phal chen): ni 'dul ba'i sems can gyi khams thams cad kyi don du dgongs pa'i skad cig re re la/bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par 'tshang rgya bar mnga' che ba ste/
18a4(phal chen): de la chos thams cad mngon par sangs rgyas pa gang yang med la/gang mngon par sangs rgyas pa la gang yang med la/gang gis mngon par sangs rgyas pa'ang med de/mngon 
18a5(phal chen): par 'tshang rgya bar 'gyur ba med kyang slob pa'i sa la gnas shing /mngon par 'tshang rgya bar yang mi brjod pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad kyang sgrib pa med par rtogs par 
18a6(phal chen): thugs su chud pas/sems can thams cad gdul ba'i don du ye shes tshad med pa'i tshul gyis sangs rgyas kyi rnam par 'phrul ba chen po'i yul yongs su rgyun mi gcod pa 'di ni 
18a7(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi mnga' chen pos mnga' bsgyur ba'i chos bdun pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni spyan gyi skye mched kyi sangs 
18a8(phal chen): rgyas kyi phrin las gang bsgrub pa de snyan gyi skye mched kyis yongs su grub par mdzad do//snyan gyi skye mched kyi sangs rgyas kyi phrin las gang ba sgrub pa de shangs kyi skye mched kyis 
18b1(phal chen): yongs su grub par mdzad do//shangs kyi skye mched kyis sangs rgyas kyi phrin las gang bsgrub pa de          ljags kyi skye mched kyis yongs su grub par mdzad do//ljags kyi skye mched 
18b2(phal chen): kyis sangs rgyas kyi phrin la sa gang bsgrub pa de ni sku'i skye mched kyis yongs su sgrub par mdzad do//sku'i skye mched kyis sangs rgyas gyi phrin las gang bsgrub pade thugs kyi skye mched 
18b3(phal chen): kyis yongs su grub par mdzang do//thugs kyi skye mched kyis sangasargyas kyi phrin las bsgrub pa da 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad kyi dmigs pa 'jig rten pa dang /'jig rten 
18b4(phal chen): las 'das pa sna tshogs rnam par phye ba thams cad grub par mdzad do//de bzhin du dmigs pa thams cad re re yang sbrel bzhir bsgrub pas sangs rgyas kyi phrin lasabasgrub pa la mnga' 
18b5(phal chen): che ba ste/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi mnga' chen pos mnga' bsgyur ba'i chos brgyad pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni rang gi sku'i ba spu'i bu ga gcig gi nang 
18b6(phal chen): du sems can gyi khams ma lus pa tham sa cad gzung ba mdzad cing khongs su chud par mdzad pa dang /sems can re re zhing yang lus sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod du med pa tsam du yangs par   
18b7(phal chen): mdzad kyang sems can de dag la gnod par mi 'gyur ba dang /de dag du yang sems can re re zhing 'jig rten gyi khams gcig tu brgyud pas bskal pa grangs med pa zad par gnas par 
18b8(phal chen): bya ba dang /'jig rten gyi khams grangs med par gom pa gcig gis phyin pas/'jig rten gyi khams de dag du sangs rgyas byung ba rnams kyi spyan sngar 'gro bar bya ba dang /sangs rgyas de dag 
19a1(phal chen): @#//byung ba'i tshe yang brjod du med pa'i 'jig rten gyis khams gyi rgyud dang /gling de dag na sems can gyi gtso bo brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa'i chos kyi 
19a2(phal chen): 'khor lo bskor bar bya ba dang /ba spu'i bu ga de nas sangs rgyas gcig nas gcig tu brgyud pas brjod du med pa'i yang ba rjod du med pa 'byung bar bya ba dang /sems can gcig nas 
19a3(phal chen): gcig tu brgyud pa brjod du med pa 'byung bar ba dang /chos kyi 'khor lo gcig nas gcig tu brgyu ba brjod du med pa'i yang rjod du med pa bskor bar bya ba dang /chos kyi dbyings bstan 
19a4(phal chen): pa gcig nas gcig tu rgyud pa brjod du med pa'i yang brdzod du med pa shin tu bstan par bya ba dang /'das pa gcig nas gcig tu brgyudab brjod du med pa dang /ma 'ongs pa 
19a5(phal chen): gcig nas gcig tu brgyudapa brjod du med pa dang /da ltar byung ba gcig nas gcig tu brgyad pa brjod du med pa yongs su brjod par bya ba dang /nam mkha'i dbyings mtha' dang dbus 
19a6(phal chen): med par bstan pa kun tu mtshams med par sems can thams cad 'ong zhing 'gro ba dang /skye ba'i skye mched thams cad du 'dod pa'i longs spyod rgya chen po gegs med pa thams cad 
19a7(phal chen): 'byung bar bya ba la mnga' che ba ste/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi mnga' chen pos mnga' bsgyur ba'i chos dgu pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das
19a8(phal chen): rnams ni chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa thams cad pad mos brgyan pa'i seng ge'i khri pad mo'i snying po can la de bzhin gshegs pa gcig gi chos kyi dbyings tshad med pa thams cad du byas 
19b1(phal chen): pa'i skus byang chub tu mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas te/sangs rgyas kyi rnam par 'phrul pa thams cad yongs su bstan par mdzad pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa gcig gis ji lta bar 
19b2(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi khams sems can kyi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyi sems can gyi gtso bo thams cad kyang de bzhin du dgongs pa skad cig ma re re'i byin gyi rlabs kyis brlab pa dang /dgongs pa 
19b3(phal chen): skad cig la ji lta ba de bzhin du dgongs pa skang cig ma thams cad la yang /pad mo'i rgyud kyis yongs su dag par mngon par 'tshang rgya bar bya ba dang /de bzhin du chos kyi dbyings thams 
19b4(phal chen): cad ma lus pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod du med pa rnam par dag pa/rgyan sna tshogs dang ldan pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams su dmigs pa rnam pa sna tshogs dang ldan pa'i zhing rnam par dag pa 
19b5(phal chen): 'byam klas pa/brjod du med pa mtshan nyid tha dad pa rnam pa tha dad pa'i rgyud tshad med cing 'byam klas pa/dus kyi rgyud grangs med pa dang ldan pa bstan pa ma 'dres pa dang ldan pa/blo tshad med pa dang 
19b6(phal chen): mos pa shin tu yangs pa dang ldan pa/mtshan nyid sna tshogs kyi gnas med pa/mtshan nyid bsam gyis mi khyab pa dang ldan pa/tshad med pa thams cad dgongs pa skad cig ma las dgongs pa skad 
19b7(phal chen): gcig ma gcig gis thams cad yongs su bstan kyang /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ched du bsgrub pa mi mdzad pa la mnga' che ba ste/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi 
19b8(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi mnga' chen pos mnga' bsgyur ba'i gos bcu pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag gis sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi mnga' chen pas mnga' bsgyur ba'i 
20a1(phal chen): @#//chos so//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi chos yongs su rdzogs pa tshad med pa bsam gyi mi khyab pa rnams te/
20a2(phal chen): bcu gang zhe na/mtshan re re zhing bsod nams brgya ba'i mtshan yongs su dag par 'gyur ba 'di ni yongs su rdzogs pa dang po ste/des sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams byang chub tu mngon 
20a3(phal chen): par 'tshang rgya'o//sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams yongs su rdzogs pas/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams byang chub tu mngon par 'tshang rgya'o//dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad yongs su rdzogs 
20a4(phal chen): pas sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dis rnams byang chub tu mngonapar 'tshang rgya'o//yon tan thams cad kyi 'byung gnas phun sum tshogs pa'i spyod pa yongs su dag pa yongs su rdzogs 
20a5(phal chen): pas/sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams byang chub tu mngon/sems can yongs su smin par bya ba dang /gdul ba yongs su rdzogs pas sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas
20a6(phal chen): rnams byang chub tu mngon bar 'tshang rgya'o//sangs rgyasakyi mnga' 'gro ba thams cad kyis zil gyi mi non pa/yongs su rdzogs pas sangs chos ldan 'das rnams byang chub tu
20a7(phal chen): mngon par 'tshang rgya'o/zhing yongs su dag par bya ba yongs su rdzogs pas sams rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams byang chung tu mngon par 'tshang rgya'o //thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes 
20a8(phal chen): yongs su rdzogs pas sangs sargyas bcom ldan 'das rnam byang chub tu mngon par 'tshang rgya'o//mtshan dang dpe byad bzang po dang gzugs kyi sku blta ba don yod pa yongs su rdzogs
20b1(phal chen): pas sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams byang chub tu mngon par 'tshang rgya'o//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad dang mnyam pa nyid yongs su rdzogs pas sangs rgyas bcom ldan
20b2(phal chen): 'das rnams byang chub tu mngan par 'tshang rgya ste/sangs rgyas kyi phrin las yongs su rdzogs par mdzad nas yongs su mya ngan las 'da'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni sangs 
20b3(phal chen): rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnamasakyi sangs rgyas kyi chos yongs su rdzogs pa tshad med pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras pa bcu po 'di dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'a
20b4(phal chen): rnams kyi thabs mkhas pa ste/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams chos thams cad byin gyis brlabs pa med cing spros pa thams cad med par yang thugs su
20b5(phal chen): chud la/dge ba'i sgo nas sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi brlabs kyang shin tu ston cing brjod de/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi thabs mkhas pa dang po'o/
20b6(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam ni chos thams cad la gzigs pa med cing phan tshun du mkhyen pa med do//ma bcings ma grol ba dang gzung ba med cing bsags pa med de/
20b7(phal chen): yongs su rdzogs pa med par yang thugs su chud la sangs rgyas kyis chos thams cad la mnga' dam pa'ang brnyes te/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dis rnams chos thams cad la mnga'i
20b8(phal chen): bsgyur ba de yang /yang dag pa de kho na nyid ma nor ba rnam pa gzhan du mi 'gyur ba dang /chos thams cad bdag gi med cing yongs su 'dzin pa med la de kho na nyid kyi mtha' las rnam par mi 'da'
21a1(phal chen): @#//yang /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni mnga' chen pos chos kyi dbyings thams cad snang ba la yang /rab tu mkhyen pa 'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi/
21a2(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi thabs mkhas pa gnyis pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/rnams ni mtshan ma'i mtha' thams cad mi mnga' ste/mtshan ma thams cad dang mi 'grogs shing /mtshan ma'i 
21a3(phal chen): mngon par shes pa thams cad dang ma 'dres la de'i rang bzhin du ngo bo nyid med pa dang /ji ltar ngo bo nyid med pa'i sgra'i tshul thams cad thugs su chud pas kyang gzugs kyi mtshan ma'i sku gzugs 
21a4(phal chen): rnam par dag pa grangs med pa dang /zhing gi mtshan ma dang rnam par spyod pa'i mtshan ma zad mi shes pa rnam pa sna tshogs yang dag par bstan cing bshad pa'i ye shes kyi phung po mun pa med pa 
21a5(phal chen): 'gro ba thams cad kyi sgron ma chen por gyur pas mun pa med pa'i snang ba ston pa 'di ni sangs rgyas pa bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi thabs mkhas ba gsum pa'o//chags pa med pa'i 
21a6(phal chen): mtha' ni sngon gyi mtha' yang ma yin/phyi ma'i mtha' yang ma yin/da ltar byung ba yang ma yin te/chos kyi dbyings su gnas pa na 'das pa 'am/ma 'ongs pa dang /da ltar byung ba yang ma 
21a7(phal chen): yin pas de bzhin nyid kyis mtshan nyid kyis 'byung ba med kyang /sangs rgyas 'byung ba rnams kyi rigs tshad med pa'i phyir shin tu bstan pa brjod du med pas 'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang da ltar byung ba'i 
21a8(phal chen): sangs rgyas skye ba yang rab tu brjod cing kun tu gzigs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi yul gyis de bzhin du gzigs pa 'di ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams gyi sangs rgyas kyi thabs mkhasapa 
21b1(phal chen): bzhi pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni sku'i phrin las dang gsung gi phrin las dang /thugs kyi phrin las kyis mngon par 'du mdzad pa yang mi mnga' la/yongs su blang ba mi mnga' bas 
21b2(phal chen): bzhugs shing /rnam par phye ba med pa'i mthar thug pa ste/dor ba med pa'i chos kyi gnas kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la/bgrang ba'i tshul las rnam par 'das kyang tshul nges pa med pa'i mdzod du gyur 
21b3(phal chen): cing|bgrang dang mngon par bsgrub pa mtha' las pa dang /'jig rten pa dang /'jig rten las 'das pa rnam pa sna tshogs tha dad pa'i grangs gyi tshul thugs su chud pa ye shes sgrib pa med pas 
21b4(phal chen): gzigs pa rnams te/chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa thams cad kyi sems can gdul ba rnam par dmigs pa la/rdzu 'phrul sna tshogs tshad med mu med par mngon par bsgrub pa 
21b5(phal chen): mdzad pa 'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi thabs mkhas pa lnga pa'o//chos thams cad lta ba med pas tha mi dad pa dang bye brag med pa dang /'byung ba med pa dang mtshan 
21b6(phal chen): nyid med pa dang /sna tshogs med pa dang /blang ba med pa dang /dor ba med pa dang /rang bzhin med par rab tu mkhyen kyang /chos kyi rgyun shin tu nges pa yang dag par bsgrub 
21b7(phal chen): cing 'jig rten gyi tshul dang 'gal ba med pa dang /rang bzhIn med pa yang dag par gzigs pas thams cad mkhyen pa/thams cad gzigs pa/mkhyen par bya ba thams 
21b8(phal chen): cad ma lus par gzigs par chos la mnga' bsgyur ba thams cad kyang rab tu ston la/de bzhin nyid kyi rang bzhin las kyang mi nyams pa 'di ni sangs rgyas bco ma ldan 'das rnams kyi 
22a1(phal chen): @#//sangs rgyas kyi thabs mkhas pa drug pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni thugs su chud pa'i rnam pa gcig gis gzhan dang gzhan mkhyen par bya ba chos 
22a2(phal chen): de'i skyon med pa las rnam par ma 'das pa gnas med pa dge ba'i rtsa ba rnam par dag pa mnyam pa nyid kyi chos skyon med pas/dus thams cad du gnas pa rab tu mkhyen te/de la yang nyi ma 
22a3(phal chen): gcig gam/zla ba phyed dam/lo gcig gam/lo brgya'am/bskal pa gcig pa dang /'du bas rjes su bsnyag pa dang /phyir phyogs pa mi mnga' bar dus tshad med pa dang /nyi ma gcig dang /
22a4(phal chen): yud tsam gcig gi dus dang snga dro nas nyi ma phyed kyi dus dang /phyi dro nas snga dro'i dus dang /nyi ma gung nas phyi dro'i dus dang /nyi ma bgrangs pa'i dus dang /nyi ma bdun dang /zla ba phyed kyi dus 
22a5(phal chen): dang /zla ba dang /lo dang /lo brgya'i dus dang bskal pa gcig gi dus dang /tshad med grangs med pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i dus nas/phyi ma'i mtha'i mur thug pa'i dus su chos bstan pa dang /
22a6(phal chen): chos kyi 'khor lo thogs pa med cing phyir mi ldog/rgyun chad par mi 'gyur/bral bar mi 'gyur/bar chad par mi 'gyur/med par mi 'gyur/'jig par mi 'gyur g.yo bar mi 'gyur/ring du mi 'gyur/'khor 
22a7(phal chen): de la gnas pa nyid du yang rgyun chad par mi 'gyur te/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi thabs mkhas pa bdun pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni 
22a8(phal chen): chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa thams cad du rtag tu dus la bab par bzhugs pa ste/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/rnams ni mi 'jigs pa tshad med pa mnga' ba'o//so so yang dag par rig pa 
22b1(phal chen): grangs med pa mnga' ba'o//so so yang dag par rig pa tshad med par mnga' ba'o//so so yang /dag par rig pa zad mi shes pa mnga' ba'o//so so yan dag par rig pa thogs pa med pa mnga' ba'o//soso yang dag par rig pa mtha' yas pa mnga' ba'o//
22b2(phal chen): thun mong ma yin pa'i so so yang dag par rig ba mnga' ba'o//so so yang ngag par rig pa mthar thug pa med pa mnga' ba'o/so so yang dag par rig de kho na dang ldan pa mnga' ba'o//so so ya nga 
22b3(phal chen): dag par rig pa tshig gi tshul thams cad rab tu rtogs pa mnga' ba'o//chos thams cad so so yang dag par rig ma mnga' ba'o//bsam pa dang mos pa dang dbang po ji lta ba bzhin du chos kyi tshul dang 
22b4(phal chen): rnam grangs tshul ji ltar rnam par bkod pa bzhin du sa 'tshams med par ston te/mdo sde bye ba khrag khrig brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa rnams na de sde dang de la thog ma dang dbus kyi tshul gyis 
22b5(phal chen): yang dag par ston to//'di ni sangs rgyas pa com ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi thabs mkhas pa brgyad pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni ming med kyang ming dang rigs su byung 
22b6(phal chen): ba'i chos thams cad tshig dang yi ges shin tu sbyangs pa'i dbyangs mngon par sangs rgyas pa ste/ming 'das pa ma yin pa dang /ming ma 'ongs pa ma yin pa dang //ming de ltar byung ba ma yin pa dang /ming 
22b7(phal chen): sems can ma yin pa dang /ming zhing ma yin pa dang /ming chos ma yin pa dang ming yon tan ma yin pa dang /min byang chub sems dpa' ma yin pa dang /ming sangs rgyas ma yin pa dang /ming grangs 
22b8(phal chen): ma yin pa dang /ming grags med pa ma yin pa dang /mi skye ba ma yin pa dang /mig 'gag pa ma yin pa dang /mid med pa ma yin pa dang ming yod pa ma yin pa dang /shing gcig pa ma yin pa dang /
23a1(phal chen): @#//ming tha dad pa ma yin pa dang /ming thams cad ma yin pa dang /chos thams cad rang bzhin gyis brjod du med pa dang /phyogs med pa dang brda med pa dang /tshig gi lam med 
23a2(phal chen): pa sgra dang bral ba dang /sgra'i rnam pa thams cad mi 'byung ba/yongs su bsgrub pa med pa/byas pa dang rnam par byas pa med pa rnam pa gcig ma yin pa/rnam pa mang po ma yin pa yang thugs su 
23a3(phal chen): chud la/brjod pa dang spyod pa thams cad la yang pha rol tu phyin pa'i mchog brnyes pa dag ste/rnam par rtog pa'i yul mi mnga' ba dag go/mngon par 'du mdzad pa med pa la rab tu brtson pa dgo/
23a4(phal chen): rtogs pa dang kun tu rtog pa yang yongs su rtog pa 'gags pa dag go/spyod pa thams cad la mi gnas par spyod pa dag ste/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi 
23a5(phal chen): thabs mkhas pa dgu pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi chos thams cad kyi gzugs ma skyes pa rab tu mkhyen pa tshor ba'i spros pa med pa dang /'du shes kyi grangs med pa dang 
23a6(phal chen): 'du byed kun tu rgyu ba med pa dang /rnam par shes pa nye bar len pa med pa dang /rang bzhin gyis zhi bar btu mkhyen te/chos thams cad skye mched kyi rgyud med pa dang /khams rnams mi dmigs 
23a7(phal chen): pa dang /chos thams cad mi 'jigs pa dang /chos thams cad bskyed du med cing /rang bzhin gyis nam mkha' lta bu dang /chos thams cad dben pa dang /las med pa dang /rnam par smin pa 
23a8(phal chen): med pa dang /spyod pas 'byung ba med pa dang /ma grub pa dang /ma byung ba dang /grangs kyis mi brjod pa dang /yod pa mi yin pa dang /med pa ma yin pa dang /ma skyes pa dang ma 'gags pa dang /kun 
23b1(phal chen): nas nyon mongs pa med pa dang /rnam par byang ba med pa dang /skye ba med pa dang gnas pa med pa dang /yongs su gnas pa med pa dang /yangs su smin par byas pa dang /rnam par gtum pa med pa dang /
23b2(phal chen): sems can med pa dang /sems can med pa ma yin pa dang /srog med pa dang /srog med pa ma yin pa dang /rkyen med pa dang /rkyen med pa ma yin pa dang/rten med pa dang /rten med pa ma yin par 
23b3(phal chen): chos thams cad thugs su chud kyang chos kyi dbyings thams cad shin tu bstan pa la pha rol tu phyin pa'i dam pa brnyes pa dag ste/nges pa dang ma nges pa dang /log par nges pa'i phung po gsum   
23b4(phal chen): rnam par dgod pa rnams la stobs bcus thugs su chud pa'i ye shes mi 'jigs pa bzhis rnam par dag pa'i sangs rgyas kyi yul chen po dang ldan pas seng ge'i sgra sgrog pa 'di ni sangs rgyas bco 
23b5(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams kyi thabs mkhas pa bcu pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi thabs mkhas pa'o////shin tu rgyas pa 
23b6(phal chen): chen po'i mdo sangs rgyas rma dag cad ces bya ba theg pa chen po'i mdo//bam po gsum pa/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcupo 'di dag ni sangs rgyas bcomaldan 'das rnam+sa kyi sangs rgyas kyi 'phrin las rgya chen po tshad med 
23b7(phal chen): pa bsaM gyis mi khyab pa rnams te/de dag sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi brlabs ma gtogs par lha dang bcas pa'i 'jig rten dang /'das pa dang /ma 'ongs pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i nyan thos dang /rang 
23b8(phal chen): sangs rgyas thams cad kyis 'jug pa mi nus pa'o//bcu gang zhe na/phyogs bcu'i nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mthas gtugs pa'i chos kyi dbyings thams cad 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus 
24a1(phal chen): @#//pa thams cad du dga' ldan du skye ba bstan pa dang /byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa mu med pa dag la sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad de//kha dog tshad med pa gzi brjid tshad med pa snang ba 
24a2(phal chen): tshang med pa/dbyangs tshad med pa dbyangs kyi yan lag rnam par dag pa tshad med pa gsung brjod pas rnam par dmigs pa tshad med pa/ting nge 'dzin tshad med pa la spyod pa/shes rab dang ye shes 
24a3(phal chen): kyi yul tshad med pa mnga' ba rnams lha dang bcas pa'i 'jig rten la phan 'dogs pa mdzad de/bdud dang bcas pa tshangs pa dang bcas pa/dge sbyong dang bcas pa/bram ze'i skye dgu dang bcas 
24a4(phal chen): pa lha dang mi dang /lha ma yin du bcas pa'i 'jig rten la phun 'dogs pa mdzad do//byams pa chen po'i spyod yul thogs pa med pa dang /thabs thugs rje chen po'i sgo'i mthar thug pas sems 
24a5(phal chen): can la phan 'dogs pa mdzad//bsod nams kyi stobs dang /mnga' phun sum tshogs pa dang /dbang po rnam par sbyangs pa dang /cang shes kyi sems yongs su dag pas kyang sems can 
24a6(phal chen): rnams la phan 'dogs par mdzad do//theg pa sna tshogs rnam par dag pas kyang sems can rnams la phan 'dogs pa mdzad do//theg pa chen po yongs su rdzogs par bya bas kyang sems 
24a7(phal chen): can rnams la phan 'dogs ba mdzad do//'gro ba thams cad gnon pas kyang sems can rnamasal phan 'dogs pa mdzad de/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi 
24a8(phal chen): phrin las dang po'o//dga' ldan gyi pho brang nas 'phos pa med kyang yum gyi skur gshegs shing /byang chub sems dpa'i sgrub pa skye bar ston pa/sgyu ma lta bur yongs su spyod pa/sprul pa lta 
24b1(phal chen): lus nyid de 'dzin gyi mthar thug pa/mig lor lta bur rnam par dmigs pa/nam mkha'i yul tshad med par khyab pa/bzhed pa bzhin du skye ba yang chags pa mi mnga' ba/smig sgyu lta bur bstan pa/nyon 
24b2(phal chen): mongs pa med pa'i chos la yang dag par gzhol ba/gnas med pa'i ye shes la gnas pa/rang bzhin gyis 'dod chags dang bral bas rnam par dag pa/rgyan shin tu rgya che ba'i mdzod kyis sangs 
24b3(phal chen): rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad do//khang pa brtsegs pa rin po che las byas pa'i pho brang chen po rin po che'i rgyan mang po dang ldan pa na bzhugs pas kyang sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad do //
24b4(phal chen): rdzu 'phrul gyis kyang sangs rgyas kyi phrinalas mdzad do//yid la bya ba'i byin gyi brlabs chen pos kyang sangs rgyas kyi phring las mdzad do//rnam par 'phrul pa'i mdzod chen po yang dag par 
24b5(phal chen): bstan pas kyang sangs rgyas kyi 'phrin las mdzad do//ye shes kyi nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor chen pos kyang sangs rgyas gyi phrin las mdzad do//yum sku bskyed pa'i sku na bzhugs pas kyang sang+sa 
24b6(phal chen): rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad//sangs rgyas kyi yul gyi spyod yul chen po brnyes pas kyang sangs rgyas kyis phrin las mdzad do//sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis 'jig rten byin gyis brlabs pa 
24b7(phal chen): mtha' yas pas sangs rgyas kyi phrin las badzad do//ting nge 'dzin chen po grangs med pa la rnam par sbyong cing /snyoms par 'jug pas kyang sangs rgyas kyi srin las mdzad do//ting nge 
24b8(phal chen): 'dzin las bzhengs pa de dang de dag gis kyang sangs rgyas kyi phris las mdzad do//spyod pa'i thog ma la yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa chen po'i mthar thug pa'i bar du thogs mdzad 
25a1(phal chen): @#//dbus dang tha ma'i tshul gyis sangs rgyas kyi 'phrin las mdzad do//sa 'di la gnyis su med pas thams cad mthar phyin par byas nas tha mar 'jig rten gyi grangs 
25a2(phal chen): skye ba yongs su ston te/skye bas kyang 'jig rten thams cad du sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad do//gzhon nur gyur pas kyang 'jig rten thams chad du sangs rgyas kyi phrin

25a4(phal chen): mngon par 'tshang rgya bas kyang 'jig rten thamasas cad du sangs rgyas kyi 'phrin la mdzad do//chos kyi 'khor lo bskor bas kyang 'jig rten thams? cad du sangs rgyas kyi phra ba las mdzad do//
25a5(phal chen): thabs mkhas pa sna tshogs kyis kyang 'jig rten sna tshogs 'jig rten tshad med pa/'jig rten kun tu mtha' yas pa phyogs kyi dra ba'i rgyud dang /dus thams cad kyi 'jig rten ma lus pa

25b1(phal chen): rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi phrin las gnyis pa'o//dge bar spyod pa'i las rnam par dag cing gdung rabs thams cad du ye shes kyi tshul rnam par dag pas/sku tshe'i rgyud kyi tshul

25b4(phal chen): byung bar khong du chud par bya ba dang /dngos po thams cad shes pa la gzung bar bya ba dang /tshul khrims thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi mthar thug par yongs su rdzogs par bya ba dang /snying 
25b5(phal chen): rje chen pos 'gro ba mu med pa la rnam par blta bar bya ba dang /byams pa chen pos sems can med pa'i yul la rab tu rtogs par bya ba dang /dga' ba chen pos 'jig rten gyi khams

25b7(phal chen): byed par bya ba dang /chos kyi dbyings kyi lus ma lus par mngon par bsgrub par bya ba dang /thams chad du gnas med pa'i lus yongs su rdzogs par byed par bya ba dang /'khor thams cad 
25b8(phal chen): rnam par dag par nan tan tu byed par bya ba dang /chosabastan pa rnam par rjod par btubarjod par bya ba dang /'jig rten gyi dngos po la smod par bya ba dang /'gro ba thams cad kyi nyes pa 
26a1(phal chen): @#//la rtog par bya ba mdzad cing spyod pa dang /dge ba'i rtsa bas rnam par smin pa yang dag par bstan pa dang /ji ltar bkod pa'i tshul du sems can gdul ba dang /

26a4(phal chen): ba dang /thugs rje chen po las byung ba'i chos kyi sprin gyis mngon par sbyang ba dang /thams cad la skyob pa'i byams pa chen po bskyed pa'i cho 'phrul gsum gyis yongs su brlan pa dang /sangs 
26a5(phal chen): rgyas thams cad mngon pa dang /rnam par dmigs par btsun mo'i 'khor kyi nang du bstan pa yan chad du 'jig rten mtha' dang dbus med par sangs rgyas kyi phrin las yongs su bstan pa 
26a6(phal chen): mdzad cing /ye shes kyi mthu chen po thogs pa med pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi mngon par mkhyen pa sgrib pa med par bsgrub pa dang /chos rnam pa gsum gyi thabs mkhas pa dang

26a8(phal chen): kyi phrin las kyi mtha' zab cing chags pa med pa'i mthar thug pa'i thabs la mkhas pas sems can rnams kyi don mdzad de 'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas 
26b1(phal chen): kyi phrin las gsum pa'o//rnam par rgyal ba chen po mnga' ba rnams ni bra tsun mo'i 'khor rgyan rnam par phye ba tshad med pa dang ldan pa yid du 'ong ba spangs te/mngon par 'byung ba'i 
26b2(phal chen): spyod pa yongs su bstan pas sems can rnams 'jig rten gyi gnas las bskyod pa dang /'jig rten gyi mngon par zhen pa rnam par bzlog pa dang /'jig rten du gtogs pa thams 
26b3(phal chen): cad mi rtag par shes par bya ba dang /'jig rten gyi tshul la mngon par zhen pa rnam pa bzlog pa dang /'jig rtan gyi dga' ba la sred pa yongs su gcad pa dang /'jig rten kyis nyon 
26b4(phal chen): mongs pa byang zhing nub pa la yang dag par spyod par bya ba dang /sems can gyi don rjes su dran par bya ba'i phyir/mngon par 'byung ba'i don la rab tu gzhol ba dang /khyim pa'i tshul yongs 
26b5(phal chen): su spang ba bstan pa dang /nyon mongs pa med pa'i chos kyi sbyor ba la yang dag par gnas par bya ba dang /sngon gyi smon lam yongs su rdzogs par bya ba dang /yon tan gyi dkyil 'khor tshad 
26b6(phal chen): med pa yongs su snang bar bya ba dang /ye shes kyi dkyil 'khor chen po brnyes pas sems can rnams kyi mi shes pa'i sgrib pa rab tu bsal ba dang /bdag nyid bsod nams kyi zhing bla 
26b7(phal chen): na med par gyur par 'gro ba thams cad la bstan pa dang /dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad sangs rgyas la bskyed pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba'i nang du 'dus par 'gro ba thams cad las yang dag par 
26b8(phal chen): brjod pa dang /bdag nyid kyis ye shes kyi don gzigs par bstan pa dang /sems can rnams rnam par dag pa'i sgor brjod pa dang /sems can rnam kha na ma tho ba med pa'i sgor 
27a1(phal chen): @#//bsgrub pa dang /'jig rten thams cad du 'byung ba'i sgor btu bstan pa dang /thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi rgyal mtshan yang dag par bsgreng bas mdzad cing 
27a2(phal chen): mngon par 'byung ba ste/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi phrin las bzhi pa'o//don gcig ma yin pa mkhyen pa dang ldan pa thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes kyi mthar 
27a3(phal chen): thug pa rnams ni chos thams cad mngon par 'tshang rgya bar bya'o zhes byang chub kyi shing drung du byon te/byang chub tu mngon par 'tshang rgya bas kyang bdud kyi dpung thams cad rab tu 
27a4(phal chen): gzhom pa dang /sku'i byin gyi brlabs kyi mdzod kyis chos kyi dbyings kyi ma lus pa thams cad du rgyas par dgang ba'i phyir byin gyis rlabs zad mi shes pa'i mthar thug pas mtha' 
27a5(phal chen): rnam par spangs pa spyod pa tshad med pa rab tu brnyes pa thams cad mkhyen pa'i yul gyis rnam par rol pa'i ye shes kyi don dang ldan pa/thams cad mkhyen pa'i yon tan yang dag par bsgrub 
27a6(phal chen): pa yongs su rdzogs pa 'jig rten gyis khams ma lus pa thams cad du/gdan rnam par bkram pas yongs su rgyas pa/sangs rgyas kyi zhing ma lus pa thams cad du byang chub sems dpas 
27a7(phal chen): rnam par dmigs pa/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa dam pa thob pa rnams kyi zhal du blta ba/byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi rnam pa dang /smon lam khyad par can dang ldan pa'i byang chub 
27a8(phal chen): sems dpa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor thams cad kyis yang dag par bsam pa'i chos kyi 'khor lo zab mo rab tu bskor ba/sangs rgyas kyi yul tshad med pa brtsams pa/byang chub 
27b1(phal chen): sems dpa' brjod du med pa'i 'byung gnas su gyur pa/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa sna tshogs spyod pa la yang dag par gzhol ba/byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor kyi dkyil 'khor rnam 
27b2(phal chen): par dag pa mnga' ba 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis byin du brlob pa'i yul mnga' ba/dge ba'i rtsa ba bskyed pa'i sems can gyis rnam par dmigs pa/dge ba'i 
27b3(phal chen): rtsa ba thams cad tha mi dad pa dang ldan pa 'byung ba'i sa/byang chub sems dpa'i sbyod pa mtha' yas pa'i mthu'i gnas la yon tan gyis khyad par thams cad rab tu brnyes nas bzhugs pa/
27b4(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi rnam pa thams cad ma lus pa'i mthar thug pa/sems can gyi rnam pa thams cad dang /zhing gi rnam pa thams cad dang chos kyi rnam pa thams cad dang byang chub sems dpa'i 
27b5(phal chen): rnam pa thams cad dang yongs su smin par bya ba'i rnam pa thams cad dang dus kyi rnam pa thams cad dag /gdul ba'i rnam pa thams cad dag/sangs rgyas kyi rnam par 'phrul ba'i rnam pa 
27b6(phal chen): thams cad ma lus pa'i mtha' thug pa rnams 'gro ba ma lus pa thams cad du mos pa ji lta ba bzhin du rnam par 'tshang rgya bas sangs rgyas kyi zin las mdzad de/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom 
27b7(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi zin las lnga pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi chos kyi 'khor lo skor ba mdzad pa'i phyir mi ldog pa'i tshul gyis phyir mi ldog pa'i 'khor 
27b8(phal chen): lo dang /'jig rten thams cad kyi khong du chud par 'gyur ba'i tshul gyis tshad med pa'i 'khor lo dang /mi 'jigs pa'i seng ge'i nga ro chen po'i tshul gyis thams cad ma lus par mngon par 
28a1(phal chen): @#//sangs rgyas pa'i 'khor lo dang /chos kyi mdzod mu med pa'i sgos snang bar byed pa'i tshul gyi chos thams cad mkhyen pa'i mdzod kyi 'khor lo dang /ye shes nam mkha'i 
28a2(phal chen): dbyings ltar tshad med pa'i tshul gyis chags pa med pa'i 'khor lo dang /chosathams cad kyi dngos po med pa'i rang bzhin rab tu rtogs par bya ba'i tshul gyis sgrib pa med pa'i 'khor lo dang//
28a3(phal chen): sems can thams cad kyi chos kyi mig rnam par sbyong pa'i tshul gyis 'jig rten thams cad kyi sgron ma'i 'khor lo dang /'jig rten thams cad du rgyas par 'gengs pa'i tshul gyis thams 
28a4(phal chen): cad mkhyen pa'i chos brjod pa'i 'khor lo dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad dang mi 'gal ba'i tshul gyis sangs rgyas thams cad 'dab 'dris par gyur pa'i 'khor lo dang /de dag la 
28a5(phal chen): sogs pa rnam pa bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong du chos kyi 'khor lo bskor bas sems can thams cad gyi sems dang spyod pa'i rnam pa la/chos kyi 'khor lo bskor te/de ltar chos kyi 'khor 
28a6(phal chen): lo bskor bas kyang sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams sangs rgyas kyi phrin las bsam gyis mi khyab pa mdzad de/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da' rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi phrin las
28a7(phal chen): drug pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni grong dang grong khyer dang grong rdal ngang /rgyal po'i gnas su gshegs pas kyang sems can rnams la sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad de/mi'i
28a8(phal chen): rgyal po'i gnas dang /lha'i rgyal po'i gnas dang /klu'i rgyal po'i gnas dang /gnod sbyin gyi rgyal po'i gnas dang /dri za'i rgyal po'i gnas dang /lha ma yin gyi rgyal po'i gnas dang/nam mkha'
28b1(phal chen): lding gi rgyal po'i gnas dang /mi'am ci'i rgyal po'i gnas dang /lto 'phye chen po'i gnas dang /srin po'i rgyal po gnas dang sha za'i rgyal po'i gnas dag tu gshegs pas kyang sangs rgyas bco 
28b2(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams ni sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad de/'di lta ste/grong khyer gyi sgor byon nas g.yo ba dang /'od zer snang ba dang /long ba rnams mig 'theb ba dang /'on pa rnams sgra thos 
28b3(phal chen): bar 'gyur ba dang /smyon pa rnams dran pa thob par 'gyur ba dang /gos med pa rnams gos thob par 'gyur ba dang /sdug bsngal ba rnams bde ba thob pa dang /rol mo'i cha spyad ma dkrol ma brdungs 
28b4(phal chen): bar sgra yid du 'ong ba rang 'byin pa dang /rgyan snod na 'dug pa dang /gnas nas ma phog pa dang /ma bskyod ma dkrol bar bskyod cing dkrol ba'i sgra 'byung ba dang /bya sna tshogs mang po'i sgra skad yid 
28b5(phal chen): du 'ong ba 'byung ba dang /blta na gtang mi bra zhing mi 'thun pa med pa'i sku mthong bas kyang /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams sems can rnams la sangs rgyas kyi phrin las 'dzad do//mats+tsha 
28b6(phal chen): dang dpe' byad bzang po bstan pas kyang sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams sems can rnams las sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad de/yongs su gzigs pa dang /rnam par gzigs pa 
28b7(phal chen): dang /brkyang pa dang /bskum pas kyang sems can rnams la sangasargyas kyi phrin las mdzad do//gshegs pa dang slar bzhud pa dang /bzhengs pa dang /'chag pa dang /bzhugs pa dang /gzims pas 
28b8(phal chen): kyang sems can rnams la sar sa rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad/chos bstan pa dang /mi gsung bar bzhugs pas kyang sems can rnamasal sangs rgyas kyi phrin la mdzad do//rdzu 'phrul gyi cho 
29a1(phal chen): @#//'phrul dang /yongs su bstan pa'i cho 'phrul dang /rjes su bstan pa'i cho 'phrul gyis kyang sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams 'jig rten gyi khams kyi yul sems 
29a2(phal chen): can bsam pa sna tshogs rgya mtsho dang ldan pa 'jig rten gyi spyod pa thams cad rgyu zhing 'byung ba dang ldan pa dge ba'i rtsa ba chen pos shin tu brtan pa/sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa dang ldan pa/sngon 
29a3(phal chen): 'grogs 'grogs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' dang gnas pa'i sems can rnams la sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad do//yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams ni sku gzugs 
29a4(phal chen): blta na mi ngoms pa'o zhes bya bar gzhol ba dang /sangs rgyas rnams skye bar dkon no zhes bya bar yid ches par byed pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa mthong zhing /de bzhin gshegs pa'i 
29a5(phal chen): gsung thob pa ni bsod nams kyi chos dkar po'i 'byung gnas su gyur pa'o//zhes sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa yid la byed pa dang /'jig rten thams cad rnam par dag pa ni/de bzhin gshegs 
29a6(phal chen): pa skye ba yin no zhes bya bar khong du chud par byed pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi yon tan bsngags pa ni mtha' yas pa'o zhes bya bar yang dag par lta ba dang /ma byon pa'i sangs rgyas 'byung ba'i de bzhin 
29a7(phal chen): gshegs pa'i rigs yang dag par bsgrub pa dang /dge ba'i rtsa ba dang mos pa'i tshul thams cad kyis sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams mnyes par byed pa dang /de de bzhin gshegs 
29a8(phal chen): pa rnams ni yon tan mtha' yas pa'o zhes khong du chud pa byed pa dang /de bzhin bshegs pa ni sems can thams cad mngon tu gyur par rtogs pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa ni sems 
29b1(phal chen): can gyi ris bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i gnas su gyur pa'i sems pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa ni zhing thams cad na snang bar khong du chud pa dang /de lta bu'i dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad 'byung bas 
29b2(phal chen): 'jig rten thams cad na sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams sems can rnams la sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad do//dad par bya ba dang /phyag bya ba dang /thal mo sbyar bar byas pa dang /
