30a1(phal chen): @#//pa thams cad du sems can thams cad kyi bsam pa dang /mthu dang 'dul ba ji lta ba'i tshul las mi 'da' bar 'byung ngo//de ltar sangs rgyas kyi phrin las 
30a2(phal chen): 'jig rten thams cad kyi sems can kun gyi sems dang spyod pa dang bsam pa la gnas/dge ba rgya che ba tshad med pa dag phyir mi ldog pa'i yul dang /snying rje chen pos rab tu bskul

32a8(phal chen): gdung dang /ring bsrel byin gyis rlob ste/lha dang klu dang gnod sbyin dang /dri za dang /lha ma yin dang /nam mkha' lding dang /mi 'am ci dang /lto 'phye chen po dang /mi'i gnas thams

32b2(phal chen): tu med pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa byin gyis brlabs/de bzhin gshegs pa'i mchod rten gyi sangs rgyas kyi sku gdung 'jig rten thams cad du

32b6(phal chen): 'chad par sangs rgyas mchod pa la brtson pa'i bsam pas sbyin pa yongs su gtong bar 'gyur ro//bsod nams byed par 'gyur ro//mtho ris de dang de yongs su dag pa rab tu 'thob par

34b3(phal chen): cad kyi 'das pa bgrangs pa mtha' dag thams cad mngon par mkhyen pa dang /ma 'ongs pa bgrangs pa mtha' dgathams cad mngon par mkhyenapa dang /da ltar byung ba bgrangs pa mtha' dag

62b7(phal chen): thams cad na/de bzhin gshegs pa'i mtshan ring po che'i mtshan nyid kyis bsgrubs pa/rin po che'i rgyal po sna tshogs dang /rin po che thams cad kyi 'od zer kyi sprin rab tu 'bar bas/sangs 
62b8(phal chen): rgyas kyi tshul gzhan dang gzhan dag dang /sangs rgyas kyi rnam par 'phrul pa yongs su bstan pa'i 'od sangs rgyas kyi chos rgya mtsho thams cad du snang ba las/rin po che sna tshogs kyi sprin 
63a1(phal chen): @#//rab tu 'gyed cing /snying po byang chub thams cad kyi sgo ston pa kun tu snang bas phyi ma'i mtha'i mur thug pa'i bskal par sangs rgyas thams cad kun tu khyab pa'i sprin 
63a2(phal chen): ces bya ba mnga'o//de bzhin gshegs pa'i zhabs kyi mthil la rin po che mi phyed pa'i gzhi nor bu'i rgyal po ri rab mar me'i 'od zer dang ldan pa'i 'khor lo bsod nams kyi 'od zer las 
63a3(phal chen): byung ba'i rtsibs stong dang ldan pa/rgyan gzhan dang gzhan ston pas/chos kyi dbyings rgya mtsho thams cad du khyab pa/de bzhin gshegs pa'i mtshan kyi sprin gyi glong rin po che thams cad kyi 
63a4(phal chen): 'od zer rab tu 'bar bas shin tu brgyan pa'i snying po can sangs rgyas thams cad kyi 'od kyi dbyings kyi shin tu rgyas pa phyogs bcu kun tu 'od kyi dra bas rnam par mdzad pa/rin po che'i 'od zer 
63a5(phal chen): rab tu snang ba'i sprin gyi 'khor lo'i gnas mtha' yas pas/spos thams cad kyi dri yongs su 'gro ba'i sgron ma rin po che'i rgyal pos kun tu brgyan cing /rin po che sna tshogs kyi mtshan nyid kyis

63b1(phal chen): snang ba kun tu 'phel ba'i 'od kyi dbyibs yongs su ston cing /'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du sangs rgyas rgya mtsho'i sprin kun tu ston pa'i sprin ces bya ba mnga'o//de bzhin gshegs pa'i zhabs 
63b2(phal chen): kyi bol gyi logs g.yas pa la sangs rgyas kyi 'od rnam par snang ba thams cad rab tu ston pa'i dbyangs dang /in+d+ra nI la'i mdog rnam pa sna tshogs yongs su dag par chos kyi dbyings rgya mtsho ston 
63b3(phal chen): cing snying po byang chub kyi gdan yongs su ston pas/sangs rgyas kyi 'od kyi dbyings rin po che'i 'od zer 'dzin pa rab tu bkye bas/yongs su rgyas shing mtshan nyid gcig pas chos kyi dbyings 
63b4(phal chen): ma 'dres par rgyan gzhan dang gzhan du rnam par 'phrul pa'i 'od kyi sprin ces bya ba mnga'o/de bzhin gshegs pa'i zhabasakyi mthil rting pa logs na/de bzhin gshegs pa'i mtshan

64a2(phal chen): de bzhin gshegs pa'i zhabs la rin po che thams cad kyi 'od zer rnam par snang ba zla ba dang /rin po che'i 'od 'phro bas shin tu brgyan pa'i snying po can/in+d+ra nI la'i dra bas gzhi kun

64b6(phal chen): rin po che'i rgyal po kun tu dri zhim zhing 'od 'byung ba'i 'od zer kyi dkyil 'khor rgya mtsho'i gzhi rin po che'i 'khor lo thams cad du 'byung ba sna tshogs rin po che thams cad kyi kha dog dang mdog dang /
64b7(phal chen): 'od zer kyi 'khor lo khongs su chud pa'i gzhi 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho dang /sangs rgyas rgya mtsho khongs su chud par rnam par snang zhing 'bar bas/'jig rten gyi khams thams cad

79a2(phal chen): kyis rab tu zhugs gyur cing //bzang po'i 'byung gnas spyod pa dang //'gro kun ye shes 'khor lo spyod zhugs pa//lus kyi las la chags med cing //ngag la chags med sla dbyangs thams cad rnam dag la//sems 
79a3(phal chen): spyod chags pa med pa yis//dus gsum ma lus rgyas par khyab par byed//'di 'dra kun tu bzang po yi//spyod pa'i mchog la pha rol phyin pa thob gyur pa//brjod du med pa'i dkyil 'khor rnams//chos kyi sku 
79a4(phal chen): kun tu rab tu brjod par byed//ma 'ongs dus kyi bskal pa dang //'byam klas zhing rnams brjod du med pa thams cad kyang //skad cig sems kyis rab tu shes//chos nyid de rnams par rtog pa med//gang 
79a5(phal chen): rnams spyod pa'i sa 'di la//spyod pa'i dam pas rjes su zhugs par gyur pa rnams//byang chub sems dpa' mchog rnams kyis//yon tan spyod pa phyogs tsam bshad par bya//mtha' dang dbus med ye 
79a6(phal chen): shes can//stobs bcu rnams kyi spyod yul mchog la zhugs gyur pa//thams cad shes byed mkhas pa rnams//mi ldog spyod pas rab tu zhugs gyur cing //'od byed blo gros dam pa dang //kun tu bzang 
79a7(phal chen): po'i smon lam mchog la zhugs gyur pa/dpe zla med pa de dag gi/ye shes tshul dang spyod pa brjod par bya//rdul phran gcig gi cha shas la//'jig rten khams rnams ji snyed de dag shes pa dang //spyod 
79a8(phal chen): pa rgya che thos gyur na//'jig rten de//thams cad mos par 'gyur//rdul phran gcig la ji lta ba//de bzhin rdul phran thams cad ma lus gzung byas ste//ye shes bzang po de dag ni//'jig rten khams 
79b1(phal chen): la 'jug pa bsam mi khyab//de dag dus kyi rnam pa la'ang //rdul phran gcig gi khyon kyi cha la yang dag 'jug/rdul phran gcig la de bzhin du//zhing yangs rnam pa tha dad kun la 'jug/rdul phran thams cad re re la//zhing rnams 
79b2(phal chen): brjod du med pa mang po 'dzud pa byed//rnam pa tha dad du ma dang //gcig dang tshad med de bzhin rab tu shes//ji snyed ci'ang ma lus pa'i//chos dbyings gzung ba thams cad yang dag gzung byed cing //mtshan nyid tha dad sna 
79b3(phal chen): tshogs te//sems can grangs kyi skal ba rgyud mi mnyam//'jig rten phra bar 'jug pa la//phra la shin tu gzhol bas phra bar 'jug byas te//brjod du med par bstan pa yi//bskal pa thams cad ma lus 
79b4(phal chen): 'dzud pa byed//de dag ring dang thung ba dang //dus kyi skad cig sems la bskal pa mi 'thun shes//shes spyod mi mnyam ma yin pa'i//shes pas de dag thams cad khong du chud//yangs dang yangs min mkhas 
79b5(phal chen): pas ni//'jig rten zab mo rnams la rab tu 'jug par byed//lus la zhing rnams de shes te//zhing la 'ang de bzhin gcig min lus 'dzud do//phyogs rnams bcu pa thams cad du//phyogs rnams kun la 'jug 
79b6(phal chen): cing skad cig du ma dang //'jig rten khams ni 'byam klas la//tshad med gzi brjid ldan pa rab tu 'jug/gang yang zhing rnams mtha' yas shing //dbus med gzung ba byas shing dus kyi dbyings su 'dzud//zhing 
79b7(phal chen): gi rgyud la mkhas pa'i rnams//shes dang ldan pas ye shes rab tu 'byung //bzang po'i yon tan ldan pa'i rnams//phyogs dang 'jig rten khams kyi rgyud du gyur pa dag/ji snyed yod pa de 'jug ste//
79b8(phal chen): brjod du med pa de dag rab tu shes//zhing dang zhing gis rnams kyi//brjod du med pa'i zhing rnams kun kyang rab tu mthong //de dag tshul yang rab tu shes las rnams//gang gis sbyangs pa 'ang khong du chud//
80a1(phal chen): @#//gang yang zhing rnams du ma dag/las rnams khrag khrig kun kyis kun nas nyon mongs bar//'gro bas kun tu bsgrubs byas pa//de dag thams cad rgyud kyang ci 'dra shes//brjod du med pa 
80a2(phal chen): yangs pa'i rnams//zhing gcig bstan pas de dag yongs su shes par 'gyur//de 'dra'i zhing la 'jug pa dang //gang la grangs med de dag kun la 'jug/'jig rten khams ni gcig la yang//
80a3(phal chen): 'jig rten khams thams cad yang dag 'dzud pa byed//'jig rten gcig tu dgongs mi 'gyur//'jig rten khams rnams de bzhin 'dres mi 'gyur//spyi'u tshugs rnams dang gong ma'i rnams//
80a4(phal chen): bla dang 'og gis rnams longs su gzung byas te//'gro ba'i rnam par ming thogs pa//'jig rten kun la 'jug cing rab tu shes//snrel bzhir rgyu ba'i rgyud rnams dang //zhing rnams mtha' yas mtha' 
80a5(phal chen): dang dbus yod ma yin pa//sna tshogs yangs pa rab tu shes//rnam pa gcig shes rnam pa tha dad shes//zhing gi grangs rnams rab tu shes//ye shes chags med mtha' ldan kun gyi grangs 
80a6(phal chen): rnams kyang //bzang po'i theg pa spyod pa yis//rgyal sras kun tu bzang rnams rab tu shes//'jig rten thams cad sprul pa dang //sems can rnam par sprul dang de bzhin zhing sprul dang /
80a7(phal chen): chos rnams sprul pa'i tshul shes pa//sangs rgyas sprul pa'i pha rol phyin pa rnams//'jig rten phra zhing zab pa dang //yangs shing sna tshogs yangs pas cho ma cho dgu rnams//sna tshogs tha 
80a8(phal chen): dad rnam bsags pa//las kyi rnam smin gnas pa'i 'jig rten rnams//rdzu 'phrul rnam par 'phrul pa yis//chos dbyings 'jug par bya la shin tu bslabs pa yi//rgyal sras mtha' dang 
80b1(phal chen): dbus myed pa//phyogs bcu thams cad ma lus 'gengs pa byed//'jig rten khams rnams de dag gi/ming ni bskal pa sems can snyed kyis mthar thug cing //zad par brjod par mi nus te ming bshad gal te sangs 
80b2(phal chen): rgyas gsung na gsung //'jig rten mgon po rnams kyi yang //mtshan rnams mtshan nyid mi 'thun rnam pa sna tshogs mtshan//bskal pa brjod med zad byas kyang //mtshan rnams gcig nas gcig 
80b3(phal chen): brgyud mthar thug med//'on kyang dman pa'i sems med par//mkhas pa rnams ni dus gsum bde gshegs sangs rgyas phyir//chos kyi dbyings las byung ba ste//sangs rgyas ye shes sa la 'ang 
80b4(phal chen): rab tu zhugs//dran pas chags med dag pa ste//chags med yid dang ldan zhing blo gros mtha' yas pas//ye shes blo gros mchog ldan pa//chos dbyings bshad la pha rol phyin pa thob//gang gis 
80b5(phal chen): 'jig rten khams kyi rnams//yangs dang phra ba rnam pa sna tshogs shes gyur cing //der ni 'das pa'i dus rnams la//sems gcig bskyed pas rab tu 'jug pa byed//mi yi seng ge de dag tu//sangs rgyas 
80b6(phal chen): spyod pa sna tshogs rnam pas chags med par//byang chub mchog tu sangs rgyas shing //rnam par 'phrul pa thams cad ston pa mdzad//de bzhin ma 'ongs dus rnams kyi//rjes su gang byon de yi 
80b7(phal chen): bskal pa ci 'dra dang //de la mi yi seng ge gang //skye bar 'gyur ba de dag kun kyang shes//smon lam spyod pa ci 'dra dang //spyod yul gang rnams sgom pa gang gis grub gyur cing //sgrub pa'i rnam pa ci 'dra 
80b8(phal chen): bas//bskal pa gang la byang chub 'tshang rgya dang //de dag 'khor kyang khong du chud//sku tshe'i tshad dang sems can 'du ba ci 'dra dang //chos kyi sgo mo gang zhig gis//'gro bar chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba 
81a1(phal chen): @#//shes de ltar kun tu bzang po'i ye shes mchog gis bsgrub pa'i spyod pa'i sa gnas shing //de dag thams cad rab tu shes de ni 'gro gtso kun gyi 'byung gnas yin//da ltar byung ba gzung 
81a2(phal chen): byas shing //'jig rten khams rnams thams cad 'dab chags ma lus par//'jig rten khams rnam dag tu 'jug/de bzhin chos kyi dbyings rnams rab tu shes//de na myi yi gtso bo rnams//
81a3(phal chen): dgra las rgyal zhing thams cad gzigs pa ji snyed pa//mi thub rgol ba dpa' bo rnams//chos kyi mnga' la pha rol phyin brnyes pa//de dag 'khor kyang rab tu shes//sems can 'dul dang zhing 
81a4(phal chen): rnams rnam par dag pa dang //rgyal ba'i rnam par 'phrul ba rnams//sems dpa' bskal ba yangs pa zad par byed//skyes bu'i mchog rnams 'dul mdzad cing //'jig rten mchog tu phyin 
81a5(phal chen): pa'i byin brlabs dam pa la//mi zad mdzod kyi shes yangs pas//ye shes tshul la mkhas pa rab tu 'jug/chags med mig gis blta ba dang //chags med rna ba mngon par sgrub pa bsgrub byed 
81a6(phal chen): cing //chags pa med pa'i lus yangs dang //yangs pa'i lces kyang 'jig rten tshim par byed//dam pa'i sems la chags med de//bsam pa kun tu rnam yangs rnam par dag dang ldan//ye shes 
81a7(phal chen): 'byung gnas bdag nyid de//dus gsum sems rnams ma lus des khyab bo//sprul pa'i pha rol phyin thob cing //zhing rnams sprul pa'i brlabs par bya la bslab pa'i rnams//sems can 
81a8(phal chen): sprul la mkhas pa dag/sprul pa sprul pas 'jig rten 'dul ba byed//'du shes rnam pa du ma ste//des ni 'jig rten sna tshogs du ba lus rnams bris//de dag thams cad rab tu shes//sangs rgyas 
81b1(phal chen): ye shes tshul la rab tu 'jug/'khor rnams brjod du med pa dag/sangs rgyas chos rnams re re dag la yod pa'i rnams//kun la 'jug cing mthong bar byed//sems can rnam par 'dul ba'i mtha' 'ang med//ye shes 
81b2(phal chen): zab mo mthong bas na//ye shes nyi ma shin tu gsal bar shar gyur pa'i//phyir mi ldog pa'i spyod pa la//'jig rten 'byam klas kun tu 'jug pa byed//ji lta'i tshul du zhugs gyur cing //'jig rten 'di 
81b3(phal chen): dag thams cad rnam par bkod 'dra bar//sems can tshul bzhin rab tu 'phags//khams gsum thams cad mig yor tshul 'drar shes//'on kyang rtog dang rnam rtog myed//'jig rten mtha' dang dbus 
81b4(phal chen): med kun la rtog mi byed//rtog dang rtog med mkhas pa ste//rtog pa'i shes pa ma rnam par rtog pa med//bskal pa brjod du med pa rnams//sems bskyed gcig la yang dag 'du bar 'jug byed cing //
81b5(phal chen): 'jig rten mi kho bo 'di mthong nas//skad cig sems kyis thams cad shes par byed//brjod du med pa'i zhing rnams kyang //sems bskyed gcig gis thams cad yongs su 'da' bar byed//bskal pa 
81b6(phal chen): brjod med zad byed kyang //sgra rtse'i cha shas gcig las g.yos med//bskal pa brjod dus med pa dag/nyi ma gcig dang yid tsam gcig tu 'dzud byed kyang //ring dang zung la gzhol ba med//skad cig gcig 
81b7(phal chen): la 'jug pa'i pha rol phyin//sems kyi rnam pa 'jig rten kun//'jig rten rnam pa sems can kun gyi sems yin no//rnam pa gnyis kyi grangs su zhugs//'on kyang de dag mi gnyis rtog pa 
81b8(phal chen): med//sgyu ma'i rnam pa 'jig rten te//sems can zhing dang sangs rgyas chos kyang de bzhin no//'on kyang 'jig rten mgon po rnams//chos kyi dbyings rnams kun la gcig tu snyoms//lus rnams 
82a1(phal chen): @#//brjod du med pa yis//phyogs bcu thams cad rgyas par bkang zhing yongs su ston//'on kyang lus la gzhol ba med//chos nyid rten pa gnas med rab tu shes//'on kyang mi yi seng ge rnams//gnyis 
82a2(phal chen): la gnas pa'i shes pas byung ba ma yin te//mi gnyis pa la'ang gnas pa med//gnyis dang gnyis med chos rnams rab tu shes//ji ltar smig rgyur ba rib dang //mig yor smra snyan de bzhin rmi lam 
82a3(phal chen): 'dra ba dang //rmi lam rnam pa sprul pa ltar//'gro ba thams cad ma lus rab tu shes//de ltar spyod yul nyams med pas//sangs rgyas kun gyi rigs kyi rjes su 'byung ba yin//kun tu bzang po'i 
82a4(phal chen): ye shes dang //chos kyi dbyings la shin tu bdal ba yin//snying rje chen po mtha' yas kyang //sems can med dang mnyam pa bzhin rnams thams cad sgom//ston nas nyon mongs nyams med pa'i//
82a5(phal chen): bsam pa dag pas 'jig rten kun tu 'gro//mi zad mkha' ltar rnam par dag pa yi//rgya che shin tu yangs pa'i sa la rab tu zhugs//gsung ba'i seng ge thams cad kyi//tshul gyi dkyil 'khor thams cad 
82a6(phal chen): rjes su dran//'jig rten rab tu spros pa la//smon lam tshad med yongs su gdab pa byas pas na//de dag spyod pa yongs su dag/'jig rten khams rnams thams cad rgyas par khyab byas pa//sangs 
82a7(phal chen): rgyas rnams dang rgyal sras dang //de bzhin sangs rgyas rnams kyi chos rnams thams cad dang //'jig rten rnams dang 'jig rten chos//chags med tha dad myed pa'i tshul du shes//chos kyi sku yi 
82a8(phal chen): snying po can//de yi lus la 'jig rten thams cad gzung byas shing //'jig rten de yi lus yin par//'jig rten kun gyi de la gos pa myed//ji ltar dad pa'i chu gsal la//gzugs 
82b1(phal chen): brlan yongs su snang yang der song 'phos pa myed//de bzhin 'jig rten thams cad kyi//rjes su lus 'gro der song 'phos pa med//ji ltar 'jig rten thams cad kyi /gos pa med cing 'jig 
82b2(phal chen): rten yongs su dag pas na//lus ni mkha' dang 'dra ba ste//de yi lus rnams gang la 'ang skyes pa med//mi zad pa yi'i lus shes pas//de ni nam yang skyes shing 'gag pa med pa ste//rtag dang mi 
82b3(phal chen): rtag ma yin par//'jig rten kun la 'am rnam par dmigs pa byed//lta ba'i rnam pa rab tu 'gags//brten pa de dag yang dag blta ba ston pa byed//bdag gi med cing gnas pa med//len pa med 
82b4(phal chen): cing chos kyi dbyings dang mnyam//ji ltar sgyu mkhan mkhas pas zhing //sna tshogs mtshan nyid rnam pa tha dad gshegs 'phrul byed//'on kyang de la 'gro ba med//gang du'ang 'gro ba de bzhin mi 
82b5(phal chen): dmyigs so//sgyu mtshad med ma yin te//sgyu ma tshad kyis dmigs par bya ba 'ang ma yin no//tshad med tshad dang ldan par yang //tshogs kyi dbus su ston pa rab tu byed//de bzhin rtogs pa'i blo ldan pa//sangs 
82b6(phal chen): rgyas kun gyi dge ba'i rtsa ba rnams las byung //sems ni tshad yod ma yin te//tshad med pa la gnas pa 'ang de dag min//'du shes rnam pa gcig min te//de dag tshad med pa dang chad dang ldan pa ston//lam gyi 
82b7(phal chen): rgyud dang 'brel pa med//tshad med rnams la gnas pa 'ang de bzhin med//sangs rgyas chos rnams zab pa ste//shin tu yangs pas zab cing zhi bas de bzhin zab//zab mo sna tshogs rgyud rnams dang //mtha' 
82b8(phal chen): yas zab pa rnams kyang rab tu shes//ye shes bzang po rmongs pa med//rgyud la dri ma med pas bsam pa rnam par dag/byin kyis rlob la mkhas pas na//srog chags bye ba shin tu dga' bar byed//
83a1(phal chen): @#//dge bar byed pa'i dkyil 'khor rnams//rab tu brjod cing dge dang mi dge rnam par ston//gnas la mngon par ma chags pas//chos kyi dbyings rnams kun tu rab tu rgyu//sems can med mthar phyin 
83a2(phal chen): gyur kyang //'jig rten thams cad ma lus par//rnam par sangs rgyas mya ngan gang mi 'da'//chos rnams bgrang ba'i tshul la mkhas//sems can bgrang tshul shes kyang grangs la skyes pa med//chos 
83a3(phal chen): kyi sprin chen char 'bebs shing //'jig rten khams rnams thams cad snang bar byed//sangs rgyas byang chub ma lus la//sems bskyed gcig gis de dag rab tu 'jug/de dag phyir ldog med pa ste//sems can 
83a4(phal chen): kun kyi khams las phyir mi ldog/sangs rgyas sku ni thob gyur kyang //chos rnams 'di dag lus yon ma yin rab tu shes//lus rnams kun gyi tshul la mkhas//lus kyis rnam phye 'jig rten rab tu shes//
83a5(phal chen): sems can kun tu rgyu ba dang //bskal pa rnams dang zhing la pha rol phyin pa thob//phyogs rnams rgya mtshor ye shes rgya mtsho'i tshul la mkhas pas zhugs//lus rnams thams cad re re las//sems can 
83a6(phal chen): kun gyi lus rnams sna tshogs ji snyed dang //sangs rgyas kun ni mtha' yas pa//ye shes shin tu yangs pa de ston to//skad cig gcig gi dus tshul la//mi yi gtso bo mang po 'byung ba rab tu shes//
83a7(phal chen): bskal pa mang po brjod med du//mi yi seng ge bgrang bas mi zad 'gyur//de dag mya ngan 'da' ba yang //skad cig gcig la tshad med shin tu yangs pa 'byung //ring bsrel rnam dbye mang po yang //
83a8(phal chen): ye shes dri med phra bas ston pa byed//de bzhin ma 'ongs dus kyi yang //mi yi seng ge mtha' yas smon lam 'debs pa'i rnams//byang chub dri med sems bskyed pa//ye shes yangs pa shin tu phra 
83b1(phal chen): bas shes//bzang po'i spyod la gnas bcas shing //de bzhin dus gsum gshegs pa sems can gtso bo rnams//de snyed skyes par 'gyur ba dag/de dag thams cad ma lus rab tu shes//de ltar rab tu shes 
83b2(phal chen): pas na//mtha' dang dbus med spyod pa'i sa dang de bzhin du//ye shes 'byung gnas blo gros la//ye shes 'khor los spyod pas phyir mi ldog/ye shes phra mo rab yangs pa//stobs bcu rnams kyi spyod yul 
83b3(phal chen): mchog la de dag zhugs//bzang po rtogs pa'i blo can rnams//phyir mi ldog la 'jug pa brjod byas na//sangs rgyas rgyal ba thams cad kyi//yang dag gzhol bar bya la de dag zhugs pa na//spyod las phyir ldog 
83b4(phal chen): med pa ste//byang chub dam pa zhi bar 'tshang rgya yang //de 'dra sems rgyud gcig la yang //mtha' dang dbus med sems rnams yang dag 'dzud pa byed//shin tu yangs pa'i tshad med las//'du shes de dang 
83b5(phal chen): de la yang dag bsgrub//nyon mongs med dang nyon mongs dang //slob dang mi slob sems kyang de bzhin rab tu shes//sems rgyud de dag re re la 'ang //sems rnams brjod du med pa mngon 'tshang rgya//gcig 
83b6(phal chen): dang gnyis su shes pa myed//yang dag yongs su 'dres par 'gyur ba 'ang med pa ste//so so'i 'du shes rnam pa la//sems can de dag skyes par gyur pa yin//'di na 'dug par mthong gyur kyang //sems can brjod 
83b7(phal chen): med gzhan dang gzhan sems ldan pa'i rnams//gzhan gyi zhing phyir 'du shes kyang //'jig rten sna tshogs rnams la rab tu skye//'di 'dra'i tshul la 'jug pa la//spyod pa'i mchog gi rjes su zhugs par gyur 
83b8(phal chen): pa rnams//kun tu bzang po'i ming thob ste//'jig rten mgon po kun gyi rjes su skyes//sems can bye ba la la ni//ngan song 'grangs pa'i ming thogs mtho ris 'du shes 'gyur//la la mtho ris sa rnams kyi//
84a1(phal chen): @#//
84b1(phal chen): 'jig rten mtshan nyid tha dad thams cad rab tu shes//thams cad mkhyen pa nyid tshul gyi//sangs rgyas ye shes kun tu mtha' 'ang yas//shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo//sangs rgyas phal po che zhes bya ba las//
84b2(phal chen): kun tu bzang po'i spyod pa bstan pa'i le'u ste//bzhi bcu gnyis pa'o//sangs rgyas rmad ga cad ces bya ba shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mngon//bam po brgyad pa/bcom ldan 'das ye shes chen pos rnam par 
84b3(phal chen): sangs rgyas pa'i dgongs pa mnga' ba 'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas thams cad dang //sku gcig pa'i mnyam pa nyid brnyes pa//sangs rgyas thams cad kyi spyod pa mnyam pa nyid dang 
84b4(phal chen): ldan pa//chags pa med pa'i spyod yul mngon par mkhyen pa//ye shes kyi rang bzhin gyi chos kyi sku mnga' ba/mtshan nyid yod nas med pa'i chos la rten pa//chos kyi dbyings mi gnyis pa'i spyod yul 
84b5(phal chen): gyi yang dag par spyod pa//mtha' dang dbus med cing mtshungs pa'i mngon par 'tshang rgya ba brnyes pa//rnam par thar pa sgrib pa med pa'i spyod yul la shin tu dgyes pa//chos kyi sku nam mkha'i dbyings kyi 
84b6(phal chen): mthas klas pa mnga' ba//de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin gyi rlabs kyi pho brang khang pa brtsegs pa'i gzhal med khang de bzhin gshegs pa bzhugs pa//chos kyi dbyings kyi snying po can//'od kun nas 'byung 
84b7(phal chen): ba rgyan chen po dang ldan pa//'od dang zhing gsal ba/rnam par smad pa'i snying po can sangs rgyas skye ba chos kyi dbyings mtha' dang dbus med pa'i gnas//byang chub sems dpa'i lus las byung ba//gzhi 'od 
84b8(phal chen): zer gyi rnam par brgyan pa/byang chub sems dpa'i lus rang bzhin du gnas pas rab tu bsgrub pa//chos chen po'i gzhir gyur pa//chos kyi dbyings kun tu rgyas pa//de bzhin gshegs pa'i'o//
85a1(phal chen): @#//dang ldan pa//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mthas gtugs pa//ye shes kyi yul yang dag pa'i mtha' spyod yul chags pa med pas rnam par gsal ba na/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po

126a6(phal chen): bu'i rang bzhin spangs te me lta bur 'bar bar 'gyur ro//nor bu rin po che chen mo ma lus pa'i mthar thug pa'i 'od kyis phog na/thams cad lhag med cing ma lus par mi snang bar 'gyur ro//kye rgyal
126a7(phal chen): ba'i sras de bzhin du de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyi ye shes kyi rgya mtsho chen po la ye shes rin po che chen po mthu dang 'od shin tu che ba bzhi 
126a8(phal chen): yod de/ye shes rin po che chen po bzhi'i mthu dang 'od kyi phog na/byang chub sems dpa'i smon lam gyi bsam pas byung ba'i bsod nams kyi chu'i phung po yongs su 'grib par 'gyur te/de dag 
126b1(phal chen): yod pas de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes kyi rgya mtsho chen po gang ba dang 'grib par mi mngon no//bzhi po de dag gyang gang zhe na 'di lta ste/dpa' rlabs thams cad zhi bar byed pa'i ye shes rin po che chen po 
126b2(phal chen): dang /chos kyi rjes su 'brang zhing srid pa yongs su zad par byed pa'i ye shes rin poche chen po dang /shes rab kyi 'od chen pos shin tu snang ba'i ye shes rin po che chen bo dang /mun pa med cing snang bar byed pa 
126b3(phal chen): de bzhin gshegs pa'i mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes rin po che chen pa ste/de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye she rin po che mthu dang /gzi brjid shin tu che ba 'di bzhi yod do//kye rgyal ba'i sras de bzhin gshegs 
126b4(phal chen): pa'i ye shes rin po che che po 'di bzhi ni gzi brjid dang mthus byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyang de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shas mnyam pa nyid 'thob par 'gyur ro//kya rgyal ba'i sras/de ltar de bzhin

126b6(phal chen): dang lha ma yin gyi 'jig rten du bcas pas/mi zlogs shing bsam gyis mi khyab pa ldan ste/byang chub sems dpa' dge ba'i rtsa ba'i rba rlabs kyi shugs bsam gyis ma khyab pa de dag la de bzhin 
126b7(phal chen): gshegs pa'i yeshes rin po che chen po'i dpa' rlabs thams cad zhi bar byed pa'i 'od kyis 'og na/dge ba'i rtsa ba'i dpa' rabs kyi shugs drag po spangs nas/ting nge 'dzin la rtse gcig tu gnas par 'gyur 
126b8(phal chen): ro//chos kyi rjes su 'brang zhing sred pa zad par byed pa'i ye shes rin po che chen po'i 'od kyis 'og na/ting nge 'dzin gyi ro dang gnas rnam par spangs te/mngon par shes pa rgya chen po mngon par bsgrub 
127a1(phal chen): @#//pa'i tshul la gnas par 'gyur ro//shes rab kyi 'od chen pos shin tu snang ba'i ye shes rin po che chen po'i 'od kyis phog na mngon par shes pa rgya chen po mngon par bsgrub 
127a2(phal chen): pa'i tshul rnam par spangs te/rigs pa snang ba chen po mngon par bsgrubs pa'i tshul la gnas par 'byung ngo//mun pa med cing snang ba med pa de bzhin gshegs pa'i mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes rin 
127a3(phal chen): po che chen po'i 'od kyis thog na/rigs pa'i snang ba chen po mngon pa bsgrub pa'i tshul rnam par spangs ste/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sa mnyam pa nyid ched du bya ba yongs su zhi ba phrin las 
127a4(phal chen): lhun gyis grub par 'gyur ba rab tu thob po//kye rgyal ba'i sras ye shes rin po che chen po 'di bzhi'i 'od snang bas phog pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i ma nyam 
127a5(phal chen): pa nyid bzhi po de dga' thob par 'gyur ro//kye rgyal ba'i sras gal te de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes kyi rgya mtsho chen po'i nang na ye shes rin po che chen pa 'di bzhi med du zin na ni/chung ngu na byang

127a8(phal chen): kye rgyal ba'i sras gzhan yang 'di lta ste dper na/'og gi chu'i phung po nye bar bzung ba yan cad/'du shes med min gyi skye mched kyi srid pa'i rtse mo'i bar gyi stong gsum gyi stong chen pa'i 'jig 
127b1(phal chen): rten gyi khams thams cad nam mkha' la gnas pa ste/gzugs med pa la spyod pa la sems can gyi gnas dang /gzugs la spyod pa'i sems can gyi gnas dang 'dod pa la spyod pa'i sems 
127b2(phal chen): can gyi gnas ji snyed pa de dag thams cad nam mkha' la  gnas shing nam mkha'i dbyings rnams byung ba'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/nam mkha'i dbyings kyi thams cad du khyab pa'i phyir te/nam mkha' la

127b4(phal chen): na/nyan thos kyi ye shes sam/rang sangs rgyas kyi ye shes sam/byang chub sems dpa'i ye shes sam/'dus byas la spyod pa'i ye shes sam/'dus ma byas la spyod pa'i ye shes 
127b5(phal chen): gang ji snyed yod pa de dag thams cad de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes kyi nam mkha' la gnas shing /de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye she kyi nam mkha' las byung ba'o//de ci'i phyi zhe na/de bzhin

127b7(phal chen): par 'gyur ba yang yod do//kye rgyal ba'i sras khong du chud par bya ba'i sgo drug pa 'dis kyang /byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs

128a2(phal chen): brtan pa'o//sman gyi rgyal po de'i rtsa ba'i 'od las/'dzam bu'i gling na yod pa'i shing thams cad kyi rtsa ba chags par 'gyur te/sdong po'i 'od kyis sdong po chags par 'gyur ro//yal

128b1(phal chen): pas byung ba/thabs mkhas pa bstan zhing sra la mi g.yo ba'i sdong po can/pha rol tu phyin pas chos kyi dbyings rgyas par 'gengs pa'i yal ga can bsam gtan dang /rnam par thar pa dang /

128b8(phal chen): cad kyi lo ma 'dzin to//me tog gi 'od las mtshan dang dpe byad bzang po/dge ba'i rtsa ba sna tshogs kyis grub pa'i me tog 'dzin to//'bras bu'i 'od las ma skyes ma byung ba'i bzod pa

129a2(phal chen): pa de ni gnyis nas mi skye ste/gnyis gang zhe na/'di lta ste/'dus ma byas kyi g.yang du ltung ba nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas rnams dang /dbang po dang bsam pa nyams pa'i

129b3(phal chen): te/ma tshig pa yod par 'gyur ba ni gnas med skabs med do//smras pa kye rgyal ba'i sras me'i phung po de'i nang gi rtsa'i zegs ma 'ga' zhig ma tshig pa ni yod kyang srid kyi/de bzhin gshegs 
129b4(phal chen): pa'i ye shes kyi ni dus gsum na yod pa'i sems can bgrangs pa dang /bskal pa bgrangs pa dang /chos bgrangs pa gang yang thugs su ma chud pa rnyed par mi rung ste/de'i gnas ma yin no//kye rgyal

131b1(phal chen): thams cad kyi bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du nye bar 'tsho bar byed pa zhig yod na/kye rgyal ba'i sras de bzhin du de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes tshad med pa'i ye shes chags pa med pa'i ye shes sems can thams cad nye bar

131b3(phal chen): pa'i ye shes de ni byis pa 'du shes su 'dzin pas/rnam par glags pa rnams mi shes shing khong du mi chud la mngon du mi byed nyams su myong bar mi byed de/de nas de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shis chags
